```text
  baselineBundleDigest,
  pairwiseDenominatorDigest,
  peerCellManifestDigest,
  commonCostBoundaryDigest,
  workloadEquivalenceClassDigest
]))))
```

`orderedRolesForKind` contains every and only roles on that physical row whose
obligation kind matches, in role order. It is non-empty. A fixed+immediate
multi-role row therefore owns one wave execution row; a predecessor+anchor row
owns distinct final and wave rows. Two logical obligations may consume one
physical result only when their full execution-key bytes and kind are
identical. Different operation, candidate, physical index, kind, denominator,
manifest, cost boundary, or class always produces separate evidence. Sharing
never shrinks a denominator, removes a role obligation, or duplicates a
subject in one Williams batch.

Sort execution rows by physical index. Define:

```text
orderedPhysicalQualificationExecutionRowsDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-ordered-physical-qualification-executions/1",
    qualificationKind,
    roleQualificationObligationProjectionDigest,
    canonicalGroupPartitionDigest,
    groupIndex,
    orderedPhysicalQualificationExecutionRows
  ]))))
```

Grouping uses unique execution-key bytes. No timing, environment observation,
prior verdict, expected speed, or author choice may enter this projection.

### 16.2 Comparable-peer claims versus operation close

All terminal dispositions stay visible, literal, non-PASS, and launch no
T8/T9. None becomes GREEN, FASTER, debt clearance, or evidence for a different
obligation. Replace §15.4's unconditional whole-tranche RED mapping only as
follows:

- `NOT_APPLICABLE` or `INCOMPARABLE` on a registered-predecessor role yields
  literal `REGISTERED_NONCOMPARABLE_NONPASS`. It is excluded from the
  qualified-comparable-peer conjunction, proves no peer win, remains in the
  close matrix, and does not alone make the operation or tranche RED.
- `UNAVAILABLE_ARTIFACT`, `UNSUPPORTED_ABI`, or
  `NO_COMMON_DENOMINATOR` on an otherwise required predecessor or anchor stays
  evidence `BLOCKED` and prevents that comparable obligation from closing.
- `NOT_APPLICABLE` or `INCOMPARABLE` on a fixed or immediate anchor is
  `ANCHOR_RED`; it cannot discharge the mandatory anchor close.
- No terminal role may discharge, replace, transfer to, or erase another
  required comparable peer or anchor role.

For each operation and required environment, derive the external peer state in
this strict precedence from registered-predecessor roles only:

1. `EXTERNAL_PEER_EVIDENCE_BLOCKED` if any such otherwise required role is
   `UNAVAILABLE_ARTIFACT`, `UNSUPPORTED_ABI`, or `NO_COMMON_DENOMINATOR`;
2. `COMPARABLE_PEER_NOT_BEATEN` if any executable predecessor is not final
   `FASTER`;
3. `ALL_COMPARABLE_PEERS_FASTER` if one or more executable predecessors exist
   and every one is final `FASTER`; or
4. `NO_QUALIFIED_EXTERNAL_PEER_NO_CLAIM` if there is no executable
   predecessor and every registered predecessor is `NOT_APPLICABLE` or
   `INCOMPARABLE`, including an exact empty predecessor set.

The fourth state is neither peer PASS nor peer RED. A new or no-qualified-peer
CSS-L4 door may close the **operation** only when both fixed and immediate
anchor wave states are GREEN with no retained AMBER/RED debt, its semantic
gate is GREEN, and every frozen BR-9 absolute throughput/latency,
deterministic-work/limit, allocation/retention, scaling, and required-
environment gate passes. It emits no faster-than-LIVE/deposed/C14 claim. Any
qualified external peer remains mandatory and cannot be hidden by this path.

The required-environment peer-claim vector contains exact rows
`[e,environmentClassDigest,externalPeerClaimState]` in close-vector order.
Define:

```text
orderedRequiredEnvironmentExternalPeerClaimRowsDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-required-environment-external-peer-claims/1",
    benchmarkQualificationContextDigest,
    closeEnvironmentVectorDigest,
    orderedRequiredEnvironmentExternalPeerClaimRows
  ]))))
```

Its final aggregate state uses the same precedence across environments:
`EXTERNAL_PEER_EVIDENCE_BLOCKED` if any row is blocked,
`COMPARABLE_PEER_NOT_BEATEN` if any row is not beaten,
ALL_COMPARABLE_PEERS_FASTER only if every row has that state, and
NO_QUALIFIED_EXTERNAL_PEER_NO_CLAIM only if every row has that state. Any
other mixture is structural RED; report-only environments never enter it.

### 16.3 Exact required-environment tagged union and vector

Environment availability is decided after the physical partition and before
T7 resource admission. It is independent of peer disposition and timing. For
each zero-based `e` in the frozen non-empty
`requiredEnvironmentClassDigests`, form exactly one evidence array:

```text
AVAILABLE evidence = [
  "v-pi-required-environment-available-evidence/1",
  closeEnvironmentVectorDigest,e,environmentClassDigest,
  environmentProbeReceiptDigest,runtimeAbiAdmissionEvidenceDigest,
  requiredControlsEvidenceDigest,"PRESENT_PRE_T7"
]

UNAVAILABLE evidence = [
  "v-pi-required-environment-unavailable-evidence/1",
  closeEnvironmentVectorDigest,e,environmentClassDigest,
  unavailabilityReasonCode,attemptedProvisionEvidenceDigest,
  ownerReviewedUnavailabilityDigest,"UNAVAILABLE_PRE_T7"
]
```

`unavailabilityReasonCode` is exactly `MACHINE_NOT_PRESENT`,
`RUNTIME_CLASS_NOT_PROVISIONABLE`, or
`REQUIRED_CONTROL_NOT_PROVISIONABLE`. Each named evidence field is a non-empty
lowercase SHA-256 digest whose content is retained in the selected artifact
store. AVAILABLE evidence proves that observed class bytes reproduce the
frozen environment class, the qualification runtime ABI can launch, and the
required pre-T7 controls exist. It does not prove or replace the per-batch T7
`statisticsResourceAdmissionReceiptDigest`. UNAVAILABLE evidence proves the
pre-observation provision attempt and owner review. Timing, a convenience
choice, report-only hardware, or a post-launch failure cannot select a branch.

Hash the chosen evidence array as `environmentAvailabilityEvidenceDigest`,
then hash exactly one receipt:

```text
AVAILABLE receipt = [
  "v-pi-required-environment-availability/1","AVAILABLE",
  closeEnvironmentVectorDigest,e,environmentClassDigest,
  peerCellPartitionBindingDigest,environmentAvailabilityEvidenceDigest,
  "T7_RESOURCE_ADMISSION_REQUIRED"
]

UNAVAILABLE receipt = [
  "v-pi-required-environment-availability/1","UNAVAILABLE_ENVIRONMENT",
  closeEnvironmentVectorDigest,e,environmentClassDigest,
  peerCellPartitionBindingDigest,environmentAvailabilityEvidenceDigest,
  "NO_T8_OR_T9"
]
```

`requiredEnvironmentAvailabilityReceiptDigest[e]` is the SHA-256 of that
array. The ordered vector rows are exactly:

```text
[e,environmentClassDigest,availabilityTag,
 requiredEnvironmentAvailabilityReceiptDigest[e]]
```

They have the same non-zero length, indices, and class order as the close
vector, with no duplicate class or receipt. Define:

```text
requiredEnvironmentAvailabilityVectorDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-required-environment-availability-vector/1",
    closeEnvironmentVectorDigest,
    peerCellPartitionBindingDigest,
    orderedRequiredEnvironmentAvailabilityRows
  ]))))
```

An AVAILABLE class proceeds only when each group separately produces its T7
resource-admission receipt; failure leaves that group T7 BLOCKED without
changing availability. An UNAVAILABLE class creates no fake plan, run,
activation, T8, or T9; its close row is evidence BLOCKED. Required classes are
conjunctive. Report-only classes use a disjoint schema and cannot enter this
vector or rescue a required state.

### 16.5 Narrow pre-observation evidence-only supersession

Without candidate/corpus/semantic bytes changing, one terminal may be reviewed
again only for `EXACT_REGISTERED_ARTIFACT_RECOVERED` or
`PRE_OBSERVATION_ABI_EVIDENCE_CORRECTED`. The first requires recovered bytes
and build closure to reproduce the already frozen subject, bundle, operation,
build, and ABI receipts exactly. The second requires retained evidence that
the unchanged artifact was compatible with the unchanged frozen ABI before
any affected observation; no runtime, flag, adapter, schema, or artifact byte
changed.

This path is forbidden after any T8 attempt or timing observation for an
affected physical-cell/kind/environment under the old context. It is forbidden
for operation/applicability, semantics, projection, cost boundary, corpus,
stratum, denominator, manifest, candidate, adapter, runtime/ABI, or artifact
changes. Those remain causal-source/E-3 amendments.

First form the pre-observation authorization, which binds the complete proposed
new partition but not a downstream plan:

```text

  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-terminal-evidence-supersession-authorization/1",
    supersessionReasonCode,candidateSubjectId,candidateBundleDigest,

    newPeerCellPartitionBindingDigest,"AUTHORIZED_PRE_OBSERVATION"
  ]))))
```

The mechanical check, both independent challenges, and root review the same
old/new partition and evidence bytes. After the kind-free new context is
formed, define the downstream transition:

```text
terminalEvidenceSupersessionTransitionDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-terminal-evidence-supersession-transition/1",
    terminalEvidenceSupersessionAuthorizationDigest,
    oldBenchmarkQualificationContextDigest,
    newBenchmarkQualificationContextDigest,
    oldPeerCellPartitionBindingDigest,newPeerCellPartitionBindingDigest,
    "APPEND_ONLY_EVIDENCE_TRANSITION"
  ]))))
```

The new plan v4 binds this transition; neither the new partition nor context
binds it, so there is no reverse cycle. Every named proof/digest is non-empty
and retained. Old partitions, terminal receipts, plan contexts, and histories
remain append-only. No old attempt, seed, statistic, or verdict is reused.
Because this path is pre-observation and evidence-only, it cannot retry/redraw
a result, react to speed, or alter a denominator. Any result predicate or
missing old history is structural RED.

under harden SHA-256

### 17.1 Exact obligation, execution-cell, group, and peer indices

Section 16.1's unindexed role rows and unqualified “for each kind” execution
projection are **RETRACTED**. The fixed qualification-kind order is exactly
`["wave","final"]`; the fixed role order remains exactly
`REGISTERED_PREDECESSOR`, `FIXED_FIRST_ACCEPTED_ANCHOR`, then
`IMMEDIATELY_PREVIOUS_ACCEPTED_ANCHOR`.

First form the physical obligation seed rows in physical-census order and role
order, retaining §16.1's kind, stage, and state-family mapping. A physical seed
row is exactly:

```text
[physicalCensusIndex,role,qualificationKind,stage,stateFamily]
```

The fixed and immediate anchor registries must either both identify genuine
prior accepted baselines for this operation or both prove the first-anchor
absence under §17.2. A one-sided registry is structural RED. In the genuine-
anchor branch there are no synthetic seeds. In the first-anchor branch append,
after every physical seed, exactly these two synthetic seeds in this order:

```text
[null,"FIXED_FIRST_ACCEPTED_ANCHOR","wave",
 "AUTHOR_WAVE_THEN_RETAIN","FIRST_ANCHOR_ESTABLISHMENT"]
[null,"IMMEDIATELY_PREVIOUS_ACCEPTED_ANCHOR","wave",
 "AUTHOR_WAVE_THEN_RETAIN","FIRST_ANCHOR_ESTABLISHMENT"]
```

For the resulting complete seed array, `obligationIndex` is exactly the
zero-based array index. The replacement obligation row is:

```text
canonicalRoleQualificationObligationRow = [
  obligationIndex,physicalCensusIndexOrNull,role,qualificationKind,
  stage,stateFamily
]
```

`orderedRoleQualificationObligationRows` is exactly the seed array with its
index prepended; its length is `obligationCount`, its indices are exactly
`0..obligationCount-1`, and its order may not be recomputed by a serializer.
Its `[physicalCensusIndex,role]` projection over non-null indices equals the
complete physical-role expansion exactly once. Its null-index projection is
either the exact two first-anchor rows or empty. The projection advances to:

```text
roleQualificationObligationProjectionDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-role-qualification-obligation-projection/2",
    publicOperationId,candidateSubjectId,candidateBundleDigest,
    completePhysicalPeerCensusDigest,
    firstAnchorAbsenceReceiptDigestOrEmptyString,
    obligationCount,orderedRoleQualificationObligationRows
  ]))))
```

For every row define:

```text
canonicalObligationRowDigest = hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
  "v-pi-canonical-role-qualification-obligation-row/1",
  roleQualificationObligationProjectionDigest,
  canonicalRoleQualificationObligationRow
]))))
```

Its ordered digest array has exactly `obligationCount` entries and preserves
obligation-index order.

Only physical rows whose frozen preflight disposition is literally
`EXECUTABLE` may enter an execution-cell projection. For every distinct
`[qualificationKind,physicalCensusIndex]` having at least one non-null
obligation, form one seed. Sort seeds first by the fixed kind order and then
by physical index. `executionCellIndex` is exactly the zero-based index in
that one operation-wide seed array. The replacement execution-cell row is:

```text
canonicalQualificationExecutionCellRow = [
  executionCellIndex,physicalCensusIndex,qualificationKind,
  orderedObligationIndices,orderedRolesForKind,
  peerCellPreflightDispositionReceiptDigest,canonicalPeerCellRow,
  obligationExecutionWorkloadKeyDigest
]
```

`orderedObligationIndices` contains every and only obligation index with that
physical index and kind, in obligation-index order; `orderedRolesForKind` is
its byte-for-byte role projection. Both are non-empty. The preflight receipt
must say `EXECUTABLE`; its four-string `canonicalPeerCellRow` and workload key
must be the exact bytes proved by that receipt and the independently complete
pairwise manifest. A terminal physical row, a synthetic first-anchor row, an
empty role set, or an invented denominator cannot inhabit this schema.

Define:

```text
completeQualificationExecutionCellProjectionDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-complete-qualification-execution-cells/1",
    roleQualificationObligationProjectionDigest,
    peerCellPartitionBindingDigest,
    executionCellCount,
    orderedCanonicalQualificationExecutionCellRows
  ]))))
```

The indices are exactly `0..executionCellCount-1`. Filtering the array by
kind gives that kind's complete executable projection; an empty filter creates
no class, group, plan, wrapper, schedule, activation, pilot, or T9 object.

The existing workload-equivalence-class partition and forced consecutive
maximal 15-baseline grouping consume exactly that filtered projection. Group
indices are per kind. Define the exact kind-group vector rows, in fixed kind
order:

```text
[qualificationKind,kindGroupCount,canonicalGroupPartitionDigest,
 orderedCanonicalGroupDigests]
```

`kindGroupCount` equals the digest-array length; group indices are exactly
`0..kindGroupCount-1`. The wave and final partitions are distinct even if
their bytes happen to select the same baselines. Define:

```text
qualificationKindGroupPartitionVectorDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-qualification-kind-group-partition-vector/1",
    completeQualificationExecutionCellProjectionDigest,
    orderedQualificationKindGroupPartitionRows
  ]))))
```

Within each group, `peerIndex` is the exact zero-based position of the
four-string cell row in `orderedPeerCellsInGroup`. Define one mapping row:

```text
executionCellGroupPeerMappingRow = [
  executionCellIndex,qualificationKind,groupIndex,peerIndex,
  canonicalGroupDigestForGroupIndex,canonicalPeerCellRow
]
```

Order mapping rows by kind order, group index, then peer index. Their
execution indices equal `0..executionCellCount-1` exactly once; for each kind,
the group/peer projection equals every forced group's complete peer rows in
group/peer order. Each mapped four-string row is byte-identical to its
execution cell. No group contains a terminal or synthetic row, and no
execution cell can be omitted, duplicated, or moved to a favorable group.

```text
executionCellGroupPeerMappingDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-execution-cell-group-peer-mapping/1",
    completeQualificationExecutionCellProjectionDigest,
    qualificationKindGroupPartitionVectorDigest,
    executionCellCount,orderedQualificationKindGroupPartitionRows,
    orderedExecutionCellGroupPeerMappingRows
  ]))))
```

The group-specific `orderedPhysicalQualificationExecutionRows` is replaced by
the exact `canonicalQualificationExecutionCellRow` projection selected by its
mapping rows, in peer-index order. Its cardinality equals
`orderedPeerCellsInGroup.length`; its execution-index and four-string
projections equal that group's mapping rows byte for byte. Advance its digest
tag to `v-pi-ordered-physical-qualification-executions/2` and bind
`completeQualificationExecutionCellProjectionDigest` plus
`executionCellGroupPeerMappingDigest` immediately before the row array.

Finally, map every obligation exactly once before environment admission:

```text
EXECUTION mapping = [
  obligationIndex,"EXECUTION",qualificationKind,executionCellIndex,
  groupIndex,peerIndex,peerCellPreflightDispositionReceiptDigest
]

STATIC_TERMINAL mapping = [
  obligationIndex,"STATIC_TERMINAL",qualificationKind,null,null,null,
  peerCellPreflightDispositionReceiptDigest
]

ANCHOR_ESTABLISHMENT_PENDING mapping = [
  obligationIndex,"ANCHOR_ESTABLISHMENT_PENDING","wave",null,null,null,
  firstAnchorAbsenceReceiptDigest
]
```

An `EXECUTABLE` physical obligation uses the first row and the exact execution
cell containing its obligation index. A non-`EXECUTABLE` physical obligation
uses the second. A synthetic obligation uses the third. No other mapping tag
exists. Sort by `obligationIndex`; the keys equal
`0..obligationCount-1`, and each execution cell's obligation-index projection
equals all and only its `EXECUTION` mappings. Define:

```text
obligationResolutionMappingDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-obligation-resolution-mapping/1",
    roleQualificationObligationProjectionDigest,
    completeQualificationExecutionCellProjectionDigest,
    executionCellGroupPeerMappingDigest,
    peerCellPartitionBindingDigest,
    obligationCount,executionCellCount,
    orderedObligationResolutionMappingRows
]))))
```

For every exact mapping row define:

```text
obligationResolutionMappingRowDigest = hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
  "v-pi-obligation-resolution-mapping-row/1",
  obligationResolutionMappingDigest,obligationResolutionMappingRow
]))))
```

The ordered mapping-row digest array has exactly `obligationCount` entries in
obligation-index order.

Section 16.4's group-specific field inside the purported operation context is
retracted. The replacement context is operation-wide and exact:

```text
benchmarkQualificationContextDigest = hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
  "v-pi-benchmark-qualification-context/2",
  publicOperationId,candidateSubjectId,candidateBundleDigest,
  completePhysicalPeerCensusDigest,peerCellDispositionPartitionDigest,
  orderedExecutablePeerCellsDigest,orderedTerminalDispositionReceiptsDigest,
  peerCellPartitionBindingDigest,roleQualificationObligationProjectionDigest,
  completeQualificationExecutionCellProjectionDigest,
  qualificationKindGroupPartitionVectorDigest,
  executionCellGroupPeerMappingDigest,obligationResolutionMappingDigest
]))))
```

The group-specific `orderedPhysicalQualificationExecutionRowsDigest` remains
in that group's plan v4, not in this context. Plan v4, wrapper v1, schedule v2,
verification v2, and activation v3 retain their accepted field order and bind
the version-2 context; no version number otherwise changes.

### 17.2 Closed lawful obligation-result and source algebra

#### 17.2.1 Non-comparative first-anchor establishment

The first-anchor branch exists if and only if no prior accepted bundle is
registered for either anchor role. Define the absence receipt before the
obligation projection:

```text
firstAnchorAbsenceReceiptDigest = hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
  "v-pi-first-anchor-absence/1",
  publicOperationId,candidateSubjectId,candidateBundleDigest,
  acceptedDoorHistoryDigest,fixedAnchorRegistryDigest,
  immediateAnchorRegistryDigest,fixedAnchorRegistryAbsenceProofDigest,
  immediateAnchorRegistryAbsenceProofDigest,
  "NO_PRIOR_ACCEPTED_BUNDLE"
]))))
```

Both absence proofs are non-empty, retained, and reviewed at T0/T1. No
duration, ratio, expected speed, peer result, or timing observation may enter
them. After this candidate's semantic gate is GREEN, form exact proposed fixed
and immediate registry rows naming this candidate and then:

```text
firstAnchorEstablishmentReceiptDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-first-anchor-establishment/1",
    firstAnchorAbsenceReceiptDigest,publicOperationId,
    candidateSubjectId,candidateBundleDigest,ownerWaveId,
    semanticGateReceiptDigest,proposedFixedAnchorRegistryRow,
    proposedImmediateAnchorRegistryRow,
    "ESTABLISHED_WITHOUT_SPEED_CLAIM"
  ]))))

firstAnchorRegistryAdvanceReceiptDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-first-anchor-registry-advance/1",
    firstAnchorEstablishmentReceiptDigest,
    fixedAnchorRegistryDigest,immediateAnchorRegistryDigest,
    nextFixedAnchorRegistryDigest,nextImmediateAnchorRegistryDigest,
    "APPEND_ONLY_FOR_NEXT_ACCEPTED_BUNDLE"
  ]))))
```

The proposed rows must name the same operation/candidate bytes and their
registered roles exactly; the next registries are their unique append-only
advances. This establishes no `GREEN`, `FASTER`, peer win, speed ratio, or
debt clearance. For this same candidate only, the exact establishment rows may
be retained through graduation. Every later accepted bundle must consume the
advanced genuine anchor registries and compare by the ordinary wave
qualification; it cannot reuse `ANCHOR_ESTABLISHED`.

For each AVAILABLE required environment define one environment establishment
receipt:

```text
firstAnchorEnvironmentEstablishmentReceiptDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-first-anchor-environment-establishment/1",
    firstAnchorEstablishmentReceiptDigest,
    firstAnchorRegistryAdvanceReceiptDigest,
    requiredEnvironmentIndex,environmentClassDigest,
    requiredEnvironmentAvailabilityReceiptDigest,
    "BOTH_ANCHOR_OBLIGATIONS_ONE_RECEIPT_NO_SPEED_CLAIM"
  ]))))
```

The fixed and immediate synthetic obligations for that environment consume
this same receipt. No other obligation or environment may consume it.

#### 17.2.2 Exact source tags and receipts

For every required environment and every obligation, exactly one source is
selected by the strict branch order in §17.2.3. The closed source-tag set is:

```text
STATIC_TERMINAL
ENVIRONMENT_UNAVAILABLE
T7_RESOURCE_BLOCKED
PILOT_INVALID
T9_INVALID
BOOTSTRAP_REJECTION_EXHAUSTED
VALID_WAVE_STATISTICS
VALID_FINAL_STATISTICS
RETAINED_WAVE_STATISTICS
FIRST_ANCHOR_ESTABLISHMENT
```

These are the branch-specific refinement of §17's closed static, environment-
blocked, resource-blocked, pilot-invalid, T9-invalid (including exhaustion),
valid-statistics, and anchor-established algebra. No broader catch-all tag is
serialized.

Their exact source receipts are:

```text
STATIC_TERMINAL source = [
  "v-pi-obligation-source-static-terminal/1",
  benchmarkQualificationContextDigest,canonicalObligationRowDigest,
  requiredEnvironmentIndex,requiredEnvironmentAvailabilityReceiptDigest,
  peerCellPreflightDispositionReceiptDigest,terminalDisposition,
  terminalDispositionEvidenceDigest
]

ENVIRONMENT_UNAVAILABLE source = [
  "v-pi-obligation-source-environment-unavailable/1",
  benchmarkQualificationContextDigest,canonicalObligationRowDigest,
  requiredEnvironmentIndex,requiredEnvironmentAvailabilityReceiptDigest,
  environmentAvailabilityEvidenceDigest,"NO_T7_T8_OR_T9"
]
```

Resource admission failure is a reviewed pre-activation result, not a runtime
or speed observation. Select the first true code in this fixed order:
`SCRATCH_RESERVATION_FAILED`, `SCRATCH_TOUCH_FAILED`,
`ARTIFACT_STORE_VERIFICATION_FAILED`. Define:

```text
statisticsResourceAdmissionFailureReceiptDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-statistics-resource-admission-failure/1",
    qualificationRunId,environmentClassDigest,batchPlanDigest,
    environmentQualifiedBatchPlanDigest,batchScheduleDigest,
    mechanicalScheduleVerificationDigest,statisticsResourcePlanDigest,
    maximumScratchBytes,maximumRetainedTraceBytes,
    durableArtifactStoreDigest,resourceAdmissionFailureCode,
    resourceAdmissionAttemptEvidenceDigest,
    "BLOCKED_PRE_ACTIVATION_NO_T8_T9"
  ]))))

T7_RESOURCE_BLOCKED source = [
  "v-pi-obligation-source-t7-resource-blocked/1",
  benchmarkQualificationContextDigest,canonicalObligationRowDigest,
  requiredEnvironmentIndex,requiredEnvironmentAvailabilityReceiptDigest,
  batchPlanDigest,environmentQualifiedBatchPlanDigest,batchScheduleDigest,
  mechanicalScheduleVerificationDigest,statisticsResourcePlanDigest,
  statisticsResourceAdmissionFailureReceiptDigest
]

PILOT_INVALID source = [
  "v-pi-obligation-source-pilot-invalid/1",
  benchmarkQualificationContextDigest,canonicalObligationRowDigest,
  requiredEnvironmentIndex,requiredEnvironmentAvailabilityReceiptDigest,
  batchActivationReceiptDigest,pilotLaunchReceiptDigest,
  pilotFinalizationAuthorityReceiptDigest,pilotFinalizationDecisionReceiptDigest,
  pilotPanelReceiptDigestOrEmptyString,pilotFailureReceiptDigest,
  pilotTerminalReceiptDigest,pilotWalDigest,"PILOT_INVALID"
]

T9_INVALID source = [
  "v-pi-obligation-source-t9-invalid/1",
  benchmarkQualificationContextDigest,canonicalObligationRowDigest,
  requiredEnvironmentIndex,requiredEnvironmentAvailabilityReceiptDigest,
  batchActivationReceiptDigest,pilotTerminalReceiptDigest,
  t9FinalizationAuthorityReceiptDigest,t9FinalizationDecisionReceiptDigest,
  t9FailureReceiptDigest,t9TerminalReceiptDigest,t9ExecutionReceiptDigest,
  t9WalDigest,"T9_INVALID"
]

BOOTSTRAP_REJECTION_EXHAUSTED source = [
  "v-pi-obligation-source-bootstrap-rejection-exhausted/1",
  benchmarkQualificationContextDigest,canonicalObligationRowDigest,
  requiredEnvironmentIndex,requiredEnvironmentAvailabilityReceiptDigest,
  batchActivationReceiptDigest,pilotTerminalReceiptDigest,
  t9FinalizationAuthorityReceiptDigest,t9FinalizationDecisionReceiptDigest,
  t9FailureReceiptDigest,t9TerminalReceiptDigest,t9ExecutionReceiptDigest,
  bootstrapRejectionExhaustionReceiptDigest,t9WalDigest,
  "BOOTSTRAP_REJECTION_EXHAUSTED"
]

VALID_WAVE_STATISTICS source = [
  "v-pi-obligation-source-valid-wave-statistics/1",
  benchmarkQualificationContextDigest,canonicalObligationRowDigest,
  requiredEnvironmentIndex,requiredEnvironmentAvailabilityReceiptDigest,
  batchActivationReceiptDigest,pilotTerminalReceiptDigest,
  t9ExecutionReceiptDigest,peerStatisticsArtifactDigest,
  batchStatisticsArtifactDigest,"CURRENT_WAVE","VALID"
]

VALID_FINAL_STATISTICS source = [
  "v-pi-obligation-source-valid-final-statistics/1",
  benchmarkQualificationContextDigest,canonicalObligationRowDigest,
  requiredEnvironmentIndex,requiredEnvironmentAvailabilityReceiptDigest,
  batchActivationReceiptDigest,pilotTerminalReceiptDigest,
  t9ExecutionReceiptDigest,peerStatisticsArtifactDigest,
  batchStatisticsArtifactDigest,"CURRENT_FINAL","VALID"
]

RETAINED_WAVE_STATISTICS source = [
  "v-pi-obligation-source-retained-wave-statistics/1",
  benchmarkQualificationContextDigest,canonicalObligationRowDigest,
  requiredEnvironmentIndex,requiredEnvironmentAvailabilityReceiptDigest,
  retainedOriginalResultRowDigest,retainedWaveReceiptDigest,
  retainedPeerStatisticsArtifactDigest,
  "NO_RERUN_UNCHANGED_WAVE_STATE"
]

FIRST_ANCHOR_ESTABLISHMENT source = [
  "v-pi-obligation-source-first-anchor-establishment/1",
  benchmarkQualificationContextDigest,canonicalObligationRowDigest,
  requiredEnvironmentIndex,requiredEnvironmentAvailabilityReceiptDigest,
  firstAnchorAbsenceReceiptDigest,firstAnchorEstablishmentReceiptDigest,
  firstAnchorRegistryAdvanceReceiptDigest,
  firstAnchorEnvironmentEstablishmentReceiptDigest,

]
```

`terminalDispositionEvidenceDigest` is exactly the
`dispositionEvidenceDigest` embedded in the named preflight receipt; it is not
a new review or a later observation.

Every named digest other than `pilotPanelReceiptDigestOrEmptyString` is
non-empty and retained in the owner-selected artifact store. That pilot field
follows the already controlling pilot finalization branch and is non-empty
only when that branch law creates one. `T9_INVALID` expressly excludes
bounded-rejection exhaustion; that cause must use the separate non-empty
exhaustion receipt and tag. Retained-wave evidence names only its immutable
original row, wave receipt, and peer-statistics artifact; it has no current
plan, activation, pilot, T9, or statistics run. No other empty field, omission,
`null`, source tag, or source/state pairing is lawful.

For each exact source array, `obligationResultSourceDigest` is the lowercase
SHA-256 of its UTF-8 `JSON.stringify` bytes. A source cannot substitute a
statistics artifact for terminal evidence or use a batch artifact for another
peer.

#### 17.2.3 Selection precedence, state effects, and exact row coverage

For one obligation/environment pair select exactly the first applicable row:

1. a `STATIC_TERMINAL` mapping selects `STATIC_TERMINAL`, independent of
   later environment or execution state;
2. otherwise an `UNAVAILABLE_ENVIRONMENT` receipt selects
   `ENVIRONMENT_UNAVAILABLE`;
3. an `ANCHOR_ESTABLISHMENT_PENDING` mapping in an AVAILABLE environment
   selects `FIRST_ANCHOR_ESTABLISHMENT`;
4. an already accepted wave row retained for graduation selects
   `RETAINED_WAVE_STATISTICS` without creating a current execution;
5. an execution group lacking the successful T7 resource-admission receipt
   selects `T7_RESOURCE_BLOCKED`;
6. its terminal invalid pilot selects `PILOT_INVALID`;
7. deterministic bounded-rejection exhaustion selects
   `BOOTSTRAP_REJECTION_EXHAUSTED`;
8. any other terminal invalid T9 selects `T9_INVALID`; or
9. only a valid T9/statistics chain selects `VALID_WAVE_STATISTICS` for a
   wave or `VALID_FINAL_STATISTICS` for a final.

Missing, multiple, out-of-order, or foreign evidence is structural RED. None
of branches 1–8 opens a retry, alternate seed, replacement environment, or
valid statistics artifact.

The exact allowed result states are:

| source | role/kind condition | obligation state | wave/final effect |
|---|---|---|---|
| `STATIC_TERMINAL` | predecessor with `NOT_APPLICABLE` or `INCOMPARABLE` | `REGISTERED_NONCOMPARABLE_NONPASS` | visible; excluded from comparable-peer count |
| `STATIC_TERMINAL` | any role with `UNAVAILABLE_ARTIFACT`, `UNSUPPORTED_ABI`, or `NO_COMMON_DENOMINATOR` | `EVIDENCE_BLOCKED` | `WAVE_BLOCKED` or final evidence-BLOCKED |
| `STATIC_TERMINAL` | anchor with `NOT_APPLICABLE` or `INCOMPARABLE` | `ANCHOR_RED` | wave/final RED |
| `ENVIRONMENT_UNAVAILABLE` | any otherwise executable/pending role | `EVIDENCE_BLOCKED` | `WAVE_BLOCKED` or final evidence-BLOCKED |
| `T7_RESOURCE_BLOCKED` | any execution role | `EVIDENCE_BLOCKED` | `WAVE_BLOCKED` or final evidence-BLOCKED |
| `PILOT_INVALID` | any execution role | `INVALID` | `WAVE_BLOCKED` or final evidence-BLOCKED |
| `T9_INVALID` | any execution role | `INVALID` | `WAVE_BLOCKED` or final evidence-BLOCKED |
| `BOOTSTRAP_REJECTION_EXHAUSTED` | any execution role | `INVALID` | `WAVE_BLOCKED` or final evidence-BLOCKED |
| `VALID_WAVE_STATISTICS` | current wave anchor | `WAVE_GREEN`, `WAVE_AMBER`, or `WAVE_RED` | the exact BR-6 wave state |
| `RETAINED_WAVE_STATISTICS` | retained wave anchor | unchanged `WAVE_GREEN`, `WAVE_AMBER`, or `WAVE_RED` | exact original wave state; no rerun |
| `VALID_FINAL_STATISTICS` | final predecessor | `FASTER`, `SLOWER`, or `INCONCLUSIVE` | the exact BR-6 final state |
| `FIRST_ANCHOR_ESTABLISHMENT` | synthetic first fixed/immediate anchor only | `ANCHOR_ESTABLISHED` | same-candidate first-anchor close only; no speed state |

No other role/kind/state combination is lawful. In particular,
`PILOT_INVALID`, `T9_INVALID`, bootstrap exhaustion, or evidence absence is never `SLOWER` or
`INCONCLUSIVE`; `ANCHOR_ESTABLISHED` is never GREEN or FASTER.

Advance the canonical row to:

```text
canonicalObligationResultRow = [
  obligationIndex,physicalCensusIndexOrNull,role,qualificationKind,
  requiredEnvironmentIndex,requiredEnvironmentAvailabilityReceiptDigest,
  obligationResolutionMappingRowDigest,sourceTag,
  obligationResultSourceDigest,obligationState
]

canonicalObligationResultRowDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-canonical-obligation-result-row/2",
    benchmarkQualificationContextDigest,canonicalObligationResultRow
  ]))))
```

Define `requiredEnvironmentCount` as exactly
`requiredEnvironmentClassDigests.length`. For each required environment,
there is exactly one row for every obligation.
The operation-wide complete result array orders environment index outermost
and obligation index innermost. Its length is exactly
`requiredEnvironmentCount * obligationCount`; its key projection
is the full Cartesian product exactly once. Define:

```text
completeObligationResultMatrixDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-complete-obligation-result-matrix/1",
    benchmarkQualificationContextDigest,
    requiredEnvironmentAvailabilityVectorDigest,
    obligationResolutionMappingDigest,obligationCount,
    requiredEnvironmentCount,
    orderedCompleteCanonicalObligationResultRowDigests
  ]))))
```

For one group/environment result, its ordered row-digest domain is exactly the
obligation-index expansion of that group's execution cells in peer-index then
obligation-index order. A static-terminal/environment result domain is exactly
the matching branch's rows in obligation-index order. A first-anchor domain is
exactly the two synthetic rows in fixed/immediate order. Result envelopes use
row **digests only**, never raw rows. The replacement envelope is exactly:

```text
benchmarkQualificationResultDigest = hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
  "v-pi-benchmark-qualification-result/2",
  benchmarkQualificationContextDigest,sourceTag,
  requiredEnvironmentIndex,qualificationKind,groupIndexOrNull,
  batchPlanDigestOrEmptyString,
  environmentQualifiedBatchPlanDigestOrEmptyString,
  batchScheduleDigestOrEmptyString,
  mechanicalScheduleVerificationDigestOrEmptyString,
  batchActivationReceiptDigestOrEmptyString,
  pilotTerminalReceiptDigestOrEmptyString,
  t9ExecutionReceiptDigestOrEmptyString,
  batchStatisticsArtifactDigestOrEmptyString,
  orderedCanonicalObligationResultRowDigests
]))))
```

The exact non-empty field cut for each tag is:

| source tag | group | plan | wrapper/schedule/verification | activation | pilot terminal | T9 execution | batch statistics |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| `STATIC_TERMINAL` | null | empty | all empty | empty | empty | empty | empty |
| `ENVIRONMENT_UNAVAILABLE` | null | empty | all empty | empty | empty | empty | empty |
| `T7_RESOURCE_BLOCKED` | index | non-empty | all non-empty | empty | empty | empty | empty |
| `PILOT_INVALID` | index | non-empty | all non-empty | non-empty | non-empty | empty | empty |
| `T9_INVALID` | index | non-empty | all non-empty | non-empty | non-empty | non-empty | empty |
| `BOOTSTRAP_REJECTION_EXHAUSTED` | index | non-empty | all non-empty | non-empty | non-empty | non-empty | empty |
| `VALID_WAVE_STATISTICS` | index | non-empty | all non-empty | non-empty | non-empty | non-empty | non-empty |
| `VALID_FINAL_STATISTICS` | index | non-empty | all non-empty | non-empty | non-empty | non-empty | non-empty |
| `RETAINED_WAVE_STATISTICS` | null | empty | all empty | empty | empty | empty | empty |
| `FIRST_ANCHOR_ESTABLISHMENT` | null | empty | all empty | empty | empty | empty | empty |

Here `empty` means the exact empty string and `null` is literal JSON `null`;
no field is omitted. Every non-empty field is the exact object already bound
by that branch's source receipt. `qualificationKind` is the common kind of all
rows. For every non-execution domain it is the kind selected by the row
filter; mixed-kind rows require separate envelopes. Version 1 is forbidden for
new results.

Order result envelopes by environment index, kind order, then this fixed scope
order: direct no-launch rows `STATIC_TERMINAL`,
`ENVIRONMENT_UNAVAILABLE`, `RETAINED_WAVE_STATISTICS`, and
`FIRST_ANCHOR_ESTABLISHMENT`, followed by execution group index ascending.
Each direct no-launch envelope contains its matching rows in obligation-index
order and has `groupIndexOrNull=null`. Within an execution group exactly one of
`T7_RESOURCE_BLOCKED`, `PILOT_INVALID`, `T9_INVALID`,
`BOOTSTRAP_REJECTION_EXHAUSTED`, `VALID_WAVE_STATISTICS`, or
`VALID_FINAL_STATISTICS` applies. Empty domains create no envelope. Their row-
digest concatenation must equal
`orderedCompleteCanonicalObligationResultRowDigests` after stable sort by
environment index then obligation index.

`benchmarkHistoryDigest` advances to version 2 and binds
`completeObligationResultMatrixDigest`, the exact ordered version-2 result-
envelope digests, and the transition chain from §17.4. The wave, final, and
close projections are mechanical filters of the complete matrix:

- `orderedWaveObligationResultRowDigests` is every `wave` obligation for every
  required environment, environment index then obligation index;
- `orderedFinalPredecessorResultRowDigests` is every
  `REGISTERED_PREDECESSOR`/`final` obligation for every required environment,
  in the same order;
- retained fixed/immediate arrays are the exact corresponding wave rows from
  their original wave receipt, with no rerun or substitution; and
- each environment close row's predecessor array is exactly that environment's
  registered-predecessor/final filter, while its two anchor fields are exactly
  that environment's fixed and immediate wave-row digests.

Every filter carries its expected key/cardinality array and proves equality to
the complete matrix. A missing, duplicate, cross-environment, cross-group,
wrong-role, wrong-kind, raw-row, or substituted digest is structural RED.
Aggregate and perfection receipts bind the version-2 history and complete
matrix transitively; they cannot select a smaller result population.

For a same-candidate first-anchor close, both exact anchor fields must be the
two `ANCHOR_ESTABLISHED` rows backed by the one per-environment establishment and registry-
advance receipt. On every later candidate they must instead be genuine
`RETAINED_WAVE_STATISTICS` rows and both must be `WAVE_GREEN` with no
AMBER/RED debt. Mixed established/statistical anchors are structural RED. The
semantic, absolute, deterministic-work, resource,
allocation/retention/scaling, and required-environment conjunction remains
mandatory in either branch.

### 17.3 External-peer close precedence over the complete result matrix

Section 16.2's external-peer state derivation is replaced by this total
precedence for each required environment. Consume every registered-
predecessor/final result row from the exact complete-matrix filter; no other
row can enter and none can be omitted.

1. `EXTERNAL_PEER_EVIDENCE_BLOCKED` if any otherwise comparable predecessor
   has `STATIC_TERMINAL`, `ENVIRONMENT_UNAVAILABLE`,
   `T7_RESOURCE_BLOCKED`, `PILOT_INVALID`, `T9_INVALID`, or
   `BOOTSTRAP_REJECTION_EXHAUSTED` evidence whose result state is
   `EVIDENCE_BLOCKED` or `INVALID`;
2. `COMPARABLE_PEER_NOT_BEATEN` if no row is evidence-blocked and at least one
   eligible `VALID_FINAL_STATISTICS` row is `SLOWER` or
   `INCONCLUSIVE`;
3. `ALL_COMPARABLE_PEERS_FASTER` if one or more eligible
   `VALID_FINAL_STATISTICS` row exists and every such row is
   `FASTER`; or
4. `NO_QUALIFIED_EXTERNAL_PEER` if zero eligible final-statistics rows exist,
   zero evidence-blocked otherwise-comparable rows exist, and every registered
   predecessor row is literal `REGISTERED_NONCOMPARABLE_NONPASS`, including
   the exact empty predecessor set.

`NOT_APPLICABLE` and `INCOMPARABLE` predecessor rows remain visible in the
close array but are excluded from both the eligible and blocked counts. They
never prove a win. A missing artifact, ABI, denominator, environment,
resource admission, valid pilot, valid T9, or valid statistics artifact is
evidence-BLOCKED, never a measured non-win. Any other mixture is structural
RED. The required-environment aggregate uses the same precedence and exact
token `NO_QUALIFIED_EXTERNAL_PEER`; report-only environments remain excluded
and cannot rescue it. The no-peer operation-close safeguards of §16.2 remain
unchanged apart from the shorter exact token.

### 17.4 Operation-wide evidence-only supersession before every observation

Section 16.5's affected-cell proof and version-1 authorization/transition are
**RETRACTED**. Evidence-only recovery is permitted only while the entire old
candidate+operation qualification context has no downstream plan/schedule,
environment-availability result, T7 admission result, activation, T8 attempt,
pilot action, T9 attempt/action, timing observation, raw sample, statistics
artifact, obligation result, history, wave/final/close receipt, or action/
closure WAL event across **all** kinds, groups, required and report-only
environments, and durable stores. The old static preflight receipts, complete
partition, T0/T1 review, and the target terminal receipt are the only admitted
old context artifacts. Once any other downstream object exists anywhere under
the old context, only the ordinary causal-source/E-3 supersession path may
change that context. Evidence recovery can never redraw an already observed
unaffected peer or replace an already published terminal result.

Freeze `orderedAllQualificationEnvironmentRows` as required-environment rows
first in close-vector order followed by report-only rows in their frozen
report vector order. Each row contains an environment-scope tag, its source
index, and environment-class digest; duplicate class/scope keys are structural
RED. For each kind in fixed kind order, each of its forced groups in group
order, and each environment row, form one no-observation row. If a kind has no
groups, use exactly one `groupIndexOrNull=null` sentinel row for each
environment so the empty namespace is still proved.

At one immutable qualification-store snapshot cut, enumerate these ten complete
namespaces for that row, in this order: plan/schedule objects, environment-
availability/wrapper objects, T7 resource-admission objects, activation/pilot-
attempt objects, T9-execution objects, action-WAL objects, closure-WAL
objects, raw-observation objects, statistics objects, and result/history/
wave/final/close objects. Each namespace proof is exactly:

Before proving rows, freeze `orderedNamespaceStoreRows` as exactly one row
`[namespaceTag,authoritativeStoreRole,authoritativeStoreDigest]` for each of
those ten tags in that order. Every control registry, WAL store, raw store,
statistics store, and result store named anywhere by the runner occurs in the
applicable row; an unlisted fallback, cache, recovery, or temporary store is
structural RED. Hash the array as
`qualificationNamespaceStoreVectorDigest` under tag
`v-pi-qualification-namespace-store-vector/1` with the old context and common
snapshot cut. `qualificationStoreSnapshotCutDigest` is a retained coordinator
receipt proving the exact immutable root of every authoritative store at one
logical cut; a later or independently sampled root cannot join the vector.

```text
operationNamespaceAbsenceProofDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-operation-namespace-absence-proof/1",
    oldBenchmarkQualificationContextDigest,qualificationStoreSnapshotCutDigest,
    qualificationKind,groupIndexOrNull,environmentScopeTag,

    namespaceRootDigest,namespaceRangeStartKey,namespaceRangeEndKey,
    completeOrderedMatchingObjectKeys,0,"EMPTY_AT_CUT"
  ]))))
```

`completeOrderedMatchingObjectKeys` must be exactly empty and its stated count
exactly zero. The store supplies a retained authenticated range/completeness
proof binding the root and cut; a path scan, affected-only prefix, cache view,
or claimed absence without completeness evidence is invalid. Namespace tags
occur in the exact order listed above.

The complete row is:

```text
]
```

Rows order by kind, group sentinel/index, environment-scope order, and source
index. Their key projection must equal the complete
kind×group-or-sentinel×environment cross-product exactly once. All proofs bind

Additionally, for each namespace/store row, form one global absence proof with

and `"ALL"`. Its range is the complete old candidate+operation context prefix,

This rejects foreign group, environment, kind, or recovery keys that no
expected cross-product row could name. Define:

```text
operationWideNoObservationProofDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-operation-wide-no-observation-proof/1",
    oldBenchmarkQualificationContextDigest,

    orderedNamespaceTags,orderedOperationGlobalNamespaceAbsenceProofDigests,

    "OPERATION_WIDE_PRE_DOWNSTREAM_EMPTY"
  ]))))
```

One operation-wide authorization may repair one or more terminal evidence rows
atomically. `orderedEvidenceRepairRows` sorts by physical census index and is

```text
[physicalCensusIndex,canonicalPeerCensusRow,
 oldPeerCellPreflightDispositionReceiptDigest,
 recoveredArtifactOrAbiEvidenceDigest,mechanicalEvidenceCheckDigest,
 newPeerCellPreflightDispositionReceiptDigest]
```

Indices are unique; every changed old/new partition row appears exactly once,
and no unchanged row appears. Both independent challenges and root review bind

Define the replacement authorization and transition:

```text
  ]))))
```

`transitionOrdinal` is the zero-based index in the operation's append-only

For the new context, every group plan of every kind binds the identical

allowed. The chain digest is mechanically derived from that array and is bound

binds the same full chain array. The new
history genesis names the authorization and transition chain; the old
partition, static terminal receipts, T0/T1 evidence, authorization, and every

verdict to import because their namespace is proved empty. No old attempt,

If any namespace count is nonzero, any downstream plan/environment/T7/result
key exists, any T8/T9 key exists, any WAL event exists, or any timing/

### 18.1 Every workload class is explicit and every group has one operation-wide locator

Section 17.1's singular
`[qualificationKind,kindGroupCount,canonicalGroupPartitionDigest,...]` row,
its vector digest, and every bare `groupIndex` address are **RETRACTED**.
One qualification kind may own zero, one, or several workload-equivalence
classes. For each kind, filter the complete execution-cell projection and
partition it by byte-identical `workloadEquivalenceClassDigest`. Order the
non-empty classes by lowercase digest bytes ascending. `classIndex` is the
zero-based position in that order. Within a class, preserve execution-cell
projection order and apply the existing forced consecutive maximal chunks of
15 baselines. `groupIndex` is zero-based **within that class**. No unlike
class may be merged, and no class may be split before the forced chunking rule
is applied.

For each class define exactly:

```text
qualificationClassPartitionRow = [
  qualificationKind,classIndex,workloadEquivalenceClassDigest,
  classExecutionCellCount,orderedExecutionCellIndices,
  canonicalGroupPartitionDigest,classGroupCount,
  orderedCanonicalGroupDigests
]

qualificationClassPartitionRowDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-qualification-class-partition-row/1",
    completeQualificationExecutionCellProjectionDigest,
    qualificationClassPartitionRow
  ]))))
```

`classExecutionCellCount` equals both index-array length and the exact number
of filtered cells with that class digest. The indices are unique and each
selects byte-identical class bytes. `classGroupCount` equals the group-digest
array length. The group partition and group digests are the existing exact
class-local receipts; their concatenated peer-cell rows equal this class's
complete execution-cell projection once, in order.

For each kind in fixed `wave`, `final` order define:

```text
qualificationKindClassPartitionRow = [
  qualificationKind,kindExecutionCellCount,classCount,kindGroupCount,
  orderedQualificationClassPartitionRowDigests
]

qualificationKindClassPartitionVectorDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-qualification-kind-class-partition-vector/2",
    completeQualificationExecutionCellProjectionDigest,
    orderedQualificationKindClassPartitionRows,
    orderedQualificationClassPartitionRows,
    orderedQualificationKindGroupLocatorRows
  ]))))
```

`classCount` is the number of class rows for that kind;
`kindExecutionCellCount` is their execution-cell-count sum; and
`kindGroupCount` is their group-count sum. An empty kind has all three counts
zero and an exact empty digest array; it creates no class or group. Across a
kind, the ordered class rows' execution-index sets are disjoint and their set
union equals the kind-filtered complete execution projection. Across both
kinds, that union equals `0..executionCellCount-1` exactly once. These set
equalities are mechanical and do not reorder the underlying cells.

The only group address is now the literal JSON array:

```text
groupLocator = [qualificationKind,classIndex,groupIndex]
```

It is unique within one candidate+operation context. In class-index then
class-local-group-index order, assign each group in a kind the exact
zero-based concatenation ordinal `kindGroupOrdinal` and form:

```text
qualificationKindGroupLocatorRow = [
  qualificationKind,kindGroupOrdinal,groupLocator,
  workloadEquivalenceClassDigest,canonicalGroupPartitionDigest,
  canonicalGroupDigestForGroupLocator
]
```

For each kind, ordinals are exactly `0..kindGroupCount-1`; locator rows are
the exact concatenation of every ordered class row's ordered groups and are
bound by the class-partition vector above. Locator, ordinal, class, partition,
and group-digest projections are mutually one-to-one. The ordinal is an order
proof, never an alternate group address. A group mapping row is:

```text
executionCellClassGroupPeerMappingRow = [
  executionCellIndex,kindGroupOrdinal,groupLocator,peerIndex,
  workloadEquivalenceClassDigest,canonicalGroupPartitionDigest,
  canonicalGroupDigestForGroupLocator,canonicalPeerCellRow
]

executionCellClassGroupPeerMappingDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-execution-cell-class-group-peer-mapping/2",
    completeQualificationExecutionCellProjectionDigest,
    qualificationKindClassPartitionVectorDigest,
    executionCellCount,orderedExecutionCellClassGroupPeerMappingRows
  ]))))
```

Rows order by kind, class index, group index, then peer index. Execution-cell
indices occur exactly once. For every class/group locator, the peer-index and
four-string projections equal that exact canonical group's full peer array;
for every execution cell, the class digest and four-string row equal its
source bytes. This is the sole execution→class→group→peer mapping. The §17
mapping digest and every mapping containing only a bare `groupIndex` are
historical version 1 and cannot address a new attempt.

The obligation resolution mapping advances to version 2. Its `EXECUTION` row
is exactly:

```text
[
  obligationIndex,"EXECUTION",qualificationKind,executionCellIndex,
  kindGroupOrdinal,groupLocator,peerIndex,
  peerCellPreflightDispositionReceiptDigest
]
```

Its terminal and establishment rows retain their exact §17 null fields. Its
digest tag is `v-pi-obligation-resolution-mapping/2` and it binds
`executionCellClassGroupPeerMappingDigest` in the position previously occupied
by the version-1 mapping digest. The complete execution-set and obligation-set
equalities remain unchanged.

The operation context advances exactly to:

```text
benchmarkQualificationContextDigest = hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
  "v-pi-benchmark-qualification-context/3",
  publicOperationId,candidateSubjectId,candidateBundleDigest,
  completePhysicalPeerCensusDigest,peerCellDispositionPartitionDigest,
  orderedExecutablePeerCellsDigest,orderedTerminalDispositionReceiptsDigest,
  peerCellPartitionBindingDigest,roleQualificationObligationProjectionDigest,
  completeQualificationExecutionCellProjectionDigest,
  qualificationKindClassPartitionVectorDigest,
  executionCellClassGroupPeerMappingDigest,obligationResolutionMappingDigest,
  registryAuthorityHistoryPrefixDigest,
  acceptedAnchorRegistryCommitDigestOrEmptyString
]))))
```

For the current first-anchor candidate, the last field is the exact empty
string and the authority prefix is the prefix that proved both registries
absent. For a later candidate, the commit field is non-empty and the authority
prefix must contain the history event that made that commit effective under
§18.6. A prefix or commit from a different operation, candidate ancestry, or
registry bytes is structural RED.

The group plan advances exactly to version 5:

```text
batchPlanDigest = hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
  "v-pi-batch-plan/5",
  qualificationKind,publicOperationId,candidateSubjectId,
  candidateBundleDigest,workloadEquivalenceClassDigest,
  qualificationKindClassPartitionVectorDigest,kindGroupOrdinal,groupLocator,
  canonicalGroupPartitionDigest,classGroupCount,
  canonicalGroupDigestForGroupLocator,orderedPeerCellsInGroup,
  pairwiseDenominatorDigest,commonCostBoundaryDigest,
  orderedSubjectArtifacts,m,cycleLength,blockCount,
  numericEncodingSchemaDigest,benchmarkQualificationContextDigest,
  orderedPhysicalQualificationExecutionRowsDigest,
  executionCellClassGroupPeerMappingDigest,
  combinedQualificationEnvironmentScopeVectorDigest,
  orderedAppliedTerminalEvidenceSupersessionTransitionDigests
]))))
```

The group-specific physical-execution digest advances to
`v-pi-ordered-physical-qualification-executions/3` and binds
`groupLocator`, `qualificationKindClassPartitionVectorDigest`, and
`executionCellClassGroupPeerMappingDigest` immediately before its unchanged
row array. Schedule version 3 replaces its bare qualification-kind locator
with `groupLocator` while retaining its remaining version-2 fields in order.
Activation version 4 replaces its bare partition/group fields with
`qualificationKindClassPartitionVectorDigest,groupLocator,
canonicalGroupPartitionDigest,canonicalGroupDigestForGroupLocator` and
retains all other version-3 fields in order. The wrapper, qualification ID,
run ID, attempts, WALs, authority, statistics, and terminal receipts obtain
the locator through plan/schedule/activation ancestry; none may bind a
different locator. Every direct field called `groupIndexOrNull` below is
replaced by `groupLocatorOrNull`.

### 18.2 Exact report-only and combined environment scope

Section 17.4's undefined `reportOnlyEnvironmentVectorDigest` and prose-only
`orderedAllQualificationEnvironmentRows` are **RETRACTED**. Required
environment rows remain exactly §16.3 and conjunctive. A report-only class is
registered before any T7/T8/T9 action with this exact receipt:

```text
reportOnlyEnvironmentRegistrationReceiptDigest =
```

Sort the frozen source pairs
`[environmentClassDigest,reportOnlyEnvironmentRegistrationReceiptDigest]`
lexicographically by their UTF-8 JSON string bytes and prepend the exact
zero-based position:

```text
reportOnlyEnvironmentRow = [
  reportOnlyEnvironmentIndex,environmentClassDigest,

]

reportOnlyEnvironmentVectorDigest =

    "v-pi-report-only-environment-vector/1",
```

The count equals the array length and the indices are exactly
`0..count-1`. Duplicate class digests or source pairs are structural RED. The

Every registration receipt and its controls evidence is retained in the
owner-selected artifact store. Report-only rows never enter required close,

Form one combined operation-scope vector. Required rows come first in
required-vector order, then report-only rows in report-vector order:

```text
required scope row = [
  environmentScopeOrdinal,"REQUIRED",requiredEnvironmentIndex,
  environmentClassDigest,closeEnvironmentVectorDigest
]

report-only scope row = [
  environmentScopeOrdinal,"REPORT_ONLY",reportOnlyEnvironmentIndex,

combinedQualificationEnvironmentScopeVectorDigest =

    "v-pi-combined-qualification-environment-scope-vector/1",
    candidateSubjectId,candidateBundleDigest,closeEnvironmentVectorDigest,

    orderedCombinedQualificationEnvironmentScopeRows
  ]))))
```

`environmentScopeOrdinal` is the exact zero-based combined-array position.
The count equals required plus report-only count. Scope tags are the closed
tokens above; required source indices/classes project byte-for-byte to the
close vector and bind its digest, while report-only source indices/classes/
receipts project byte-for-byte to their report vector. The combined vector is
therefore frozen before environment availability or T7 and is independent of
a terminal-evidence context repair. No class digest may occur twice anywhere in the
combined vector, including across scopes. The combined-vector key projection
is exactly the required/report-only union and its required prefix is non-empty.

The §17.4 operation-wide absence proof advances to version 2 and binds this
combined vector directly. Its real-group row key is
`[groupLocator,environmentScopeOrdinal]`; an empty kind uses exactly the
sentinel `[[qualificationKind,null,null],environmentScopeOrdinal]`. Replace
the old proof's separate kind/group/environment fields with
`groupLocatorOrKindSentinel,environmentScopeOrdinal,environmentScopeTag,
environmentSourceIndex,environmentClassDigest,environmentSourceReceiptDigest`.
Its store vector, every local and global namespace proof, authorization,
transition, plan v5, and history event all bind the same combined-vector
digest. The local key projection is exactly the complete
group-locator-or-empty-kind-sentinel × combined-environment-scope × ten-
namespace product. The ten global prefix proofs remain mandatory. The exact
replacement outer proof is:

```text
operationWideNoObservationProofDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-operation-wide-no-observation-proof/2",
    oldBenchmarkQualificationContextDigest,
    oldCompleteQualificationExecutionCellProjectionDigest,
    oldExecutionCellClassGroupPeerMappingDigest,
    oldCombinedQualificationEnvironmentScopeVectorDigest,
    qualificationNamespaceStoreVectorDigest,
    qualificationStoreSnapshotCutDigest,orderedNamespaceTags,
    orderedOperationGlobalNamespaceAbsenceProofDigests,

    "OPERATION_WIDE_PRE_DOWNSTREAM_EMPTY"
  ]))))
```

The authorization advances to
`v-pi-terminal-evidence-supersession-authorization/3` and binds the old

### 18.3 Stage-due obligation census and result matrices

The single all-stage `completeObligationResultMatrixDigest` and its demand for
not-yet-due rows are **RETRACTED**. The closed benchmark-stage tokens are:

```text
AUTHOR_WAVE
GRADUATION_FINAL
PERFECTION_FINAL
```

Exactly one applies to a stage close. For `AUTHOR_WAVE`, due obligations are
every and only `qualificationKind="wave"` row, including the exact two
synthetic establishment rows when first-anchor mode applies. Every `final`
obligation is not yet due. For either final token, currently due obligations
are every and only `final` predecessor. Every `wave` anchor was discharged by
an ancestor accepted `AUTHOR_WAVE` close and is referenced through that sealed
wave matrix; it is not due again and emits no new result. No other stage/mode
pairing exists.

Filter the immutable obligation projection in obligation-index order and
define:

```text
stageDueObligationRow = [
  stageDueIndex,obligationIndex,qualificationKind,role,stageEvidenceMode
]
```

`stageEvidenceMode` is `CURRENT_WAVE_OR_ESTABLISHMENT` for an author-wave row
or `CURRENT_FINAL` for a final predecessor row. `stageDueIndex` is the
zero-based due-array position. Define:

```text
benchmarkStageObligationCensusDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-benchmark-stage-obligation-census/1",
    benchmarkQualificationContextDigest,benchmarkStage,
    roleQualificationObligationProjectionDigest,obligationCount,
    stageDueObligationCount,orderedStageDueObligationRows,
    previouslyDischargedObligationCount,
    orderedPreviouslyDischargedObligationIndices,
    notYetDueObligationCount,orderedNotYetDueObligationIndices
  ]))))
```

At `AUTHOR_WAVE`, not-yet-due indices are exactly the final-obligation filter,
in obligation-index order, and the previously-discharged array is literal
`[]`. At either final stage, the previously-discharged indices are exactly the
wave-obligation filter in obligation-index order; the not-yet-due count is
zero and its array is literal `[]`. Due, previously discharged, and not-yet-
due sets are disjoint and their union equals `0..obligationCount-1` exactly
once. A not-yet-due or previously discharged obligation has no current result
row, result source, result envelope, placeholder, pending token, or failure
state. The census—not fake evidence—proves its lawful absence from current
execution.

For each due obligation and required environment, select exactly one §17.2
result source subject to these stage cuts:

- `AUTHOR_WAVE` permits current wave/establishment, static-terminal,
  unavailable, resource-blocked, pilot-invalid, T9-invalid, or exhaustion
  sources only for due wave rows;
- either final stage permits current final or the same failure/terminal
  sources only for due final predecessor rows.

`RETAINED_WAVE_STATISTICS` is forbidden as a newly emitted source, row, or
result envelope. A final stage instead references the immutable accepted wave
matrix, wave verdict, author-wave stage close, and author-wave history event
through the completion receipt below. The named close must be
`AUTHOR_WAVE`/`ACCEPTED`, contain every exact wave obligation/environment row,
and precede the final stage's prior history prefix. Required environment
indices, class digests, and the required-environment vector must equal the
ancestor accepted wave bytes; a changed vector requires a separately
authorized new wave, never final-stage substitution. No current matrix,
verdict, close, event, or prefix can become its own ancestor.

Order current stage result rows by required-environment index outermost and
`stageDueIndex` innermost. Their exact key is
`[requiredEnvironmentIndex,obligationIndex]`. The expected key array is the
Cartesian product of the required-environment indices and due-obligation
indices in that same order. Define:

```text
benchmarkStageResultMatrixDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-benchmark-stage-result-matrix/2",
    benchmarkQualificationContextDigest,benchmarkStage,
    benchmarkStageObligationCensusDigest,

    orderedStageCanonicalObligationResultRowDigests,
    orderedObservedStageResultKeys,"EXACT_STAGE_CARTESIAN_PRODUCT"
  ]))))
```

`expectedStageResultRowCount` is the exact safe JSON-integer product. Both key
arrays are byte-identical and contain the complete product once. Row count
equals the product. Result-envelope row concatenation, stably sorted to stage
key order, equals the matrix row array. A wrong-stage, not-yet-due, missing,
duplicate, foreign-environment, raw-row, or substituted digest is structural
RED.

For either final stage, form the exact forward-only completion receipt:

```text
benchmarkFinalCompletionMatrixDigest =

    orderedCurrentFinalResultRowDigests,

  ]))))
```

The accepted-wave arrays are exact filters of the sealed author-wave matrix;
the current-final arrays are exact filters of the current final-only matrix.
They are disjoint. Their stable obligation-index merge equals the full
required-environment×obligation Cartesian key array and complete row-digest
array exactly once. `expectedCompleteResultKeyCount` is the safe JSON-integer
product. The accepted wave verdict and stage close must be `WAVE_GREEN` and
`ACCEPTED` under the controlling strict law. The accepted wave history event
must occur in the current prior prefix. This receipt references prior bytes;
it creates no retained source, row, envelope, plan, attempt, statistic, or
rerun.

The result envelope advances to version 3:

```text
]))))
```

Its existing field cuts remain exact except that `RETAINED_WAVE_STATISTICS`
is forbidden for new envelopes. A direct source uses
literal `null`; an execution source uses its unique full group locator. Its
row domain is the applicable stage-due filter only. Envelope order is
environment, kind, direct-source order, then class index/group index. Version
2 and the all-stage matrix are forbidden for new stage publication.

### 18.4 Exact wave, final, aggregate, stage-close, history, and perfection schemas

All prose-only version-2 history/wave/final/aggregate/perfection advances in
§17.2.3 and every version-1 outer schema in §16.4 are **RETRACTED** for new
publication. The following schemas are the sole current outer receipts. The
dependency order is result envelopes → stage matrix → stage verdict →
operation aggregate → stage close → optional registry commit → history event
→ successor history prefix. No descendant digest may appear in an ancestor.

For an author-wave operation define the exact environment result filter rows:

```text
waveEnvironmentVerdictRow = [
  requiredEnvironmentIndex,environmentClassDigest,

benchmarkWaveVerdictDigest = hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([

]))))
```

This schema is lawful only for `AUTHOR_WAVE`. Its expected keys equal the
stage matrix keys byte for byte. Each environment row is the exact environment
filter; the two anchor fields select its fixed and immediate rows. The existing
state precedence produces only `WAVE_GREEN`, `WAVE_AMBER`, `WAVE_RED`, or
`WAVE_BLOCKED`; no final state appears.

For a final operation define:

```text
finalEnvironmentVerdictRow = [

]))))
```

This schema is lawful only for `GRADUATION_FINAL` or `PERFECTION_FINAL`.
Expected current-final keys equal the final-only stage matrix keys. Predecessor
and accepted-wave anchor filters are exact and disjoint; through the completion
matrix their union equals the environment's full obligation set. The §17.3
evidence-blocking and external-peer precedence remains unchanged. Accepted
anchor rows are direct references into the ancestor matrix, never new retained
rows or sources.

For either stage define one operation aggregate:

```text
benchmarkOperationAggregateDigest =
```

At `AUTHOR_WAVE`, the wave digest is non-empty and the final digest is the

the completion and final digests are non-empty and the wave digest is empty.
Every named
non-speed receipt is non-empty, current, and conjunctive. The operation verdict
is derived under the existing strict-close precedence; a report-only row can
never rescue it.

For a candidate-stage stream define the immutable stream identity and exact
history prefixes:

```text
benchmarkHistoryStreamIdentityDigest =

    "v-pi-benchmark-history-stream/2",
```

Genesis is the only prefix at event count zero. For a successor,
`eventOrdinal` is the zero-based event index, the prior prefix proves exactly
that many events, and the successor proves `eventOrdinal + 1`. Forks, gaps,
duplicate ordinals, alternate genesis, or removal are structural RED.

Order operations by frozen `orderedPublicOperationIds` and define:

```text
  priorBenchmarkHistoryPrefixDigest,closeEnvironmentVectorDigest,

  ownerOc1ChoiceReceiptDigest,durableArtifactStoreDigest,
  stageAcceptanceState
]))))
```

Operation IDs and close-row IDs are byte-identical and complete. Every row's
stage is the close stage through its bound census/matrix/aggregate. The closed
acceptance tokens are `ACCEPTED` and `REJECTED`. `ACCEPTED` requires every
mandatory operation verdict and all semantic, compatibility, required-
environment, absolute, deterministic-work/resource, allocation/retention/
scaling, OC-1, and retention predicates to satisfy existing strict law;
otherwise the token is `REJECTED`. The close binds only the **prior** history
prefix. It never binds its own event, successor prefix, or registry commit.

History events are exact:

```text
benchmarkHistoryEventDigest = hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
  "v-pi-benchmark-history-event/2",
  benchmarkHistoryStreamIdentityDigest,eventOrdinal,
  priorBenchmarkHistoryPrefixDigest,eventType,
  benchmarkStageCloseDigestOrEmptyString,
  benchmarkPerfectionCloseDigestOrEmptyString,
  orderedFirstAnchorRegistryCommitReceiptDigests,
  eventDisposition,durableArtifactStoreDigest
]))))
```

`eventType` is `STAGE_CLOSE` or `PERFECTION_CLOSE`. A stage event has a
non-empty stage-close digest, empty perfection-close string, zero or more
post-close commits in operation order, and `eventDisposition` equal to the
bound stage acceptance token. A perfection event has the reverse digest cut,
literal `[]` commits, and the bound tranche verdict. The event binds a close
that already bound this same prior prefix; only afterward may the successor
prefix bind the event. This is the sole history advance and has no close↔
history cycle.

After an accepted `PERFECTION_FINAL` stage close and its stage history event
has advanced the prefix, define:

```text
benchmarkPerfectionCloseDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-benchmark-perfection-close/2",
    benchmarkHistoryStreamIdentityDigest,candidateSubjectId,
    candidateBundleDigest,priorBenchmarkHistoryPrefixDigest,
    acceptedPerfectionFinalStageCloseDigest,
    acceptedPerfectionFinalHistoryEventDigest,
    closeEnvironmentVectorDigest,reportOnlyEnvironmentVectorDigest,
    orderedPublicOperationIds,
    orderedBenchmarkOperationAggregateDigests,
    compatibilityLaneReceiptDigest,ownerOc1ChoiceReceiptDigest,
    durableArtifactStoreDigest,tranchePerformanceVerdict
  ]))))
```

The prior prefix here must be the successor prefix containing the named
accepted perfection-final stage event. Operation IDs and aggregates equal the
accepted stage-close filters byte for byte. The tranche verdict is derived
under the existing strict perfection clauses. The perfection close then gets
its own `PERFECTION_CLOSE` history event and successor prefix; it cannot name
that descendant event or prefix in its own preimage.

### 18.5 First anchors are proposals until an accepted author-wave close commits them

Section 17.2.1's `firstAnchorRegistryAdvanceReceiptDigest`, every environment
receipt/source field that treats it as committed, and any pre-close registry
mutation are **RETRACTED**. The absence and establishment receipts remain
unchanged. Before results, form only this prospective proposal:

```text
firstAnchorRegistryProposalReceiptDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-first-anchor-registry-proposal/1",
    firstAnchorEstablishmentReceiptDigest,
    fixedAnchorRegistryDigest,immediateAnchorRegistryDigest,
    proposedFixedAnchorRegistryRow,proposedImmediateAnchorRegistryRow,
    proposedNextFixedAnchorRegistryDigest,
    proposedNextImmediateAnchorRegistryDigest,
    "PROSPECTIVE_NOT_COMMITTED"
  ]))))
```

The first-anchor environment establishment receipt advances to version 2 and
binds `firstAnchorRegistryProposalReceiptDigest` where version 1 bound the
advance receipt. The `FIRST_ANCHOR_ESTABLISHMENT` source advances to version 2
and makes the same replacement. Establishment rows and the author-wave matrix,
verdict, aggregate, and close may bind this proposal; none may bind a commit or
treat the proposed registries as authoritative.

Only after a successfully published `AUTHOR_WAVE` stage close with literal
`stageAcceptanceState="ACCEPTED"` may each first-anchor operation form:

```text
firstAnchorRegistryCommitReceiptDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-first-anchor-registry-commit/1",
    publicOperationId,candidateSubjectId,candidateBundleDigest,
    acceptedAuthorWaveStageCloseDigest,
    benchmarkStageOperationCloseRowDigest,
    firstAnchorRegistryProposalReceiptDigest,
    fixedAnchorRegistryDigest,immediateAnchorRegistryDigest,
    proposedNextFixedAnchorRegistryDigest,
    proposedNextImmediateAnchorRegistryDigest,
    "COMMITTED_AFTER_ACCEPTED_CLOSE_FOR_FUTURE_CONTEXT_ONLY"
  ]))))
```

The named operation close row is the exact row inside the accepted stage
close and binds both `ANCHOR_ESTABLISHED` rows in every required environment.
A rejected close, wrong stage, missing environment, mixed anchor branch, or
non-GREEN mandatory predicate cannot create a commit. The commit is a strict
descendant of the close. It is ordered into that close's later history event;
the proposed next registries become authoritative only after the successor
history prefix containing that event is durable. The current candidate's
context, rows, verdict, aggregate, and close cannot consume the commit.

A future candidate consuming either advanced registry must consume both,
bind this commit and its authority successor prefix in context version 3, and
use ordinary wave statistics. It cannot reuse `ANCHOR_ESTABLISHED`. A failed
candidate therefore leaves no accepted-registry mutation and needs no
rollback.

### 19.1 One exact group address reaches every group-specific identity

The §18 name `groupIndex` is narrowed to `classLocalGroupIndex`; its value and
ordering do not change. The §18 `kindGroupOrdinal` is renamed
`kindGroupIndex`; its value remains the zero-based concatenation index across
all classes of one qualification kind. There is no other current meaning for
either name. Define:

and kind-group-locator row. The one-to-one projection across all rows is the
exact kind/class/group census. A mismatched kind, class, local index,
kind-wide index, class partition, or group digest is structural RED. A direct
`groupIndex`, `kindGroupOrdinal`, or partition/index/digest tuple outside this
binding is forbidden in every new downstream preimage.
