### 19.2 A tagged scope disposition makes report-only execution lawful but non-gating

For each combined-scope row, before T4, form exactly one disposition:

```text
qualificationEnvironmentScopeDispositionDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-qualification-environment-scope-disposition/1",
    benchmarkQualificationContextDigest,
    qualificationAuthorityDigest,
    combinedQualificationEnvironmentScopeVectorDigest,
    environmentScopeOrdinal,environmentScopeTag,environmentSourceIndex,
    environmentClassDigest,environmentSourceReceiptDigest,
    environmentAvailabilityEvidenceDigest,
    environmentScopeDispositionState
  ]))))
```

The closed states and sources are:

| scope | state | source/effect |
|---|---|---|
| `REQUIRED` | `REQUIRED_AVAILABLE` | exact §16 required availability receipt; may form a wrapper |
| `REQUIRED` | `REQUIRED_UNAVAILABLE` | exact §16 required unavailability receipt; no wrapper or run |
| `REPORT_ONLY` | `REPORT_ONLY_AVAILABLE` | §18 registration plus retained mechanical availability evidence; may form a wrapper |
| `REPORT_ONLY` | `REPORT_ONLY_UNAVAILABLE` | §18 registration plus retained unavailability evidence; no wrapper or run |

The row's tag/index/class/source receipt must project byte-for-byte to the
named combined-scope row. Exactly one state applies. Required states also
project to the existing required availability vector. A report-only state
does not enter that vector and cannot manufacture a required availability
receipt.

Only either AVAILABLE state may form this tagged wrapper:

```text
environmentQualifiedBatchPlanDigest = hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
  "v-pi-environment-qualified-batch-plan/2",
  batchPlanDigest,benchmarkQualificationContextDigest,
  qualificationAuthorityDigest,
  groupAddressBindingDigest,
  combinedQualificationEnvironmentScopeVectorDigest,
  environmentScopeOrdinal,environmentScopeTag,environmentSourceIndex,
  environmentClassDigest,environmentSourceReceiptDigest,
  qualificationEnvironmentScopeDispositionDigest,
  qualificationId,qualificationRunId
]))))
```

`REQUIRED_AVAILABLE` carries the matching required availability receipt as
its source. `REPORT_ONLY_AVAILABLE` carries the matching report-only
registration receipt. The wrapper is invalid for either unavailable state.
The current schedule, verifier, activation, attempts, raw evidence, statistics
and terminals all bind this wrapper and therefore the exact scope tag.

For every available `[groupAddressBindingDigest,environmentScopeOrdinal]`
key, the authoritative execution-index store admits at most one T7 resource-
admission decision, at most one T8 attempt identity, and at most one T9
qualification-execution identity. The retained exact ledger row is:

```text
qualificationEnvironmentExecutionLedgerRow = [
  benchmarkQualificationContextDigest,groupAddressBindingDigest,
  environmentScopeOrdinal,qualificationEnvironmentScopeDispositionDigest,
  qualificationId,qualificationRunId,
  t7DecisionCount,t7DecisionDigestOrEmptyString,
  t8AttemptCount,pilotAttemptIdOrEmptyString,
  t9ExecutionCount,qualificationExecutionIdOrEmptyString
]
```

Each count is the safe JSON integer `0` or `1`; a later stage may move a
suffix from zero to one but never back, and no field may name a second digest.
Existing one-shot action/closure WAL and whole-block-replacement law governs
the one admitted execution. An unavailable state has all three counts zero.
The ledger is an identity/completeness index, not a retry authority.

For one benchmark stage, filter the complete group-address rows to that
stage's due qualification kind. Cross that array with every report-only scope
row in environment-scope order. The exact key is
`[reportOnlyEnvironmentIndex,qualificationKind,kindGroupIndex,
groupAddressBindingDigest]`; its environment-scope ordinal and source index
must project to the same report-only row. There is exactly one report-only
result for every key and no sentinel for an empty kind. The closed source/state
union is:

```text
REPORT_ONLY_ENVIRONMENT_UNAVAILABLE -> REPORT_ONLY_UNAVAILABLE
REPORT_ONLY_T7_BLOCKED               -> REPORT_ONLY_BLOCKED
REPORT_ONLY_PILOT_INVALID            -> REPORT_ONLY_INVALID
REPORT_ONLY_T9_INVALID               -> REPORT_ONLY_INVALID
REPORT_ONLY_BOOTSTRAP_EXHAUSTED      -> REPORT_ONLY_INVALID
REPORT_ONLY_VALID_STATISTICS         -> REPORT_ONLY_OBSERVED
```

Report-only timing uses the identical frozen raw-block, median, paired-log,
bootstrap, interval, and retention mechanics, but it cannot emit an ordinary
wave/final statistics state. Define
`v-pi-report-only-peer-statistics-artifact/1` by taking the exact
`v-pi-peer-statistics-artifact/2` field array, replacing its tag, and replacing
the terminal
`requiredWinningBlocksOrNull,peerStatisticsState,"VALID"` triple by literal
`null,"REPORT_ONLY_OBSERVATION","NO_VERDICT_EFFECT"`. Define
`v-pi-report-only-batch-statistics-artifact/1` by taking the exact
`v-pi-batch-statistics-artifact/2` field array, replacing its tag, replacing
`orderedPeerStatisticsArtifactDigests` with the byte-identically ordered
`orderedReportOnlyPeerStatisticsArtifactDigests`, and replacing terminal
`"VALID"` by `"REPORT_ONLY_OBSERVATION","NO_VERDICT_EFFECT"`. These are exact
field transformations; no field is omitted or reordered. Both families bind
the REPORT_ONLY scope disposition and wrapper through their run/activation
ancestry and retain every raw/statistical scalar. The ordinary required-result
source union rejects both report-only tags, and this report-only source union
rejects ordinary peer/batch statistics artifacts.

Define each result exactly:

```text
benchmarkReportOnlyQualificationResultDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-report-only-qualification-result/1",
    benchmarkQualificationContextDigest,benchmarkStage,
    qualificationAuthorityDigest,
    terminalEvidenceSupersessionTransitionChainDigest,
    combinedQualificationEnvironmentScopeVectorDigest,
    environmentScopeOrdinal,reportOnlyEnvironmentIndex,
    reportOnlyEnvironmentRegistrationReceiptDigest,
    qualificationEnvironmentScopeDispositionDigest,
    groupAddressBindingDigest,sourceTag,
    environmentQualifiedBatchPlanDigestOrEmptyString,
    batchScheduleDigestOrEmptyString,
    mechanicalScheduleVerificationDigestOrEmptyString,
    t7ResourceAdmissionDispositionReceiptDigestOrEmptyString,
    batchActivationReceiptDigestOrEmptyString,
    pilotTerminalReceiptDigestOrEmptyString,
    t9ExecutionReceiptDigestOrEmptyString,
    reportOnlyRawEvidenceManifestDigestOrEmptyString,
    reportOnlyBatchStatisticsArtifactDigestOrEmptyString,
    reportOnlyResultState
  ]))))
```

For unavailable, every wrapper-through-statistics field is the exact empty
string. T7-blocked requires the wrapper and exact T7 decision, represented by
the non-empty T7 disposition field, but has no activation, pilot, T9, or
statistics digest. Pilot-invalid and T9-invalid carry every
existing ancestor through their terminal point and no valid statistics
digest. Exhaustion carries the version-2 exhaustion receipt through the T9
execution descendant and no batch statistics artifact. Valid statistics
requires the complete retained raw-evidence manifest and the neutral report-
only batch artifact above. No other field cut exists.

The exact matrix and its append-only report history are:

```text
benchmarkReportOnlyResultMatrixDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-report-only-result-matrix/1",
    benchmarkQualificationContextDigest,benchmarkStage,
    qualificationAuthorityDigest,
    terminalEvidenceSupersessionTransitionChainDigest,
    reportOnlyEnvironmentVectorDigest,
    orderedStageDueGroupAddressBindingDigests,
    expectedReportOnlyResultCount,orderedExpectedReportOnlyResultKeys,
    orderedBenchmarkReportOnlyQualificationResultDigests,
    orderedObservedReportOnlyResultKeys,"EXACT_REPORT_ONLY_PRODUCT"
  ]))))

benchmarkReportOnlyEvidenceHistoryDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-report-only-evidence-history/1",
    benchmarkQualificationContextDigest,benchmarkStage,
    qualificationAuthorityDigest,
    terminalEvidenceSupersessionTransitionChainDigest,
    benchmarkReportOnlyResultMatrixDigest,
    orderedReportOnlyRawEvidenceManifestDigests,
    orderedReportOnlyBatchStatisticsArtifactDigests,
    durableArtifactStoreDigest,"REPORT_ONLY_NO_VERDICT_EFFECT"
  ]))))
```

The expected count is the safe-integer product of report-only count and the
due-kind group count; both key arrays are identical and complete once. The
raw/statistic arrays are exact projections of the matrix in key order and are
literal `[]` when the projection is empty. The matrix/history are retained
even when every row is unavailable or
non-winning. They enter the operation close row and later stage history only
as report-only evidence. They never enter the required stage matrix, peer-win
array, required environment aggregate, operation performance predicate,
compatibility/OC-1 predicate, final completion product, or perfection verdict.
Required and report-only samples, ratios, intervals, states, or artifacts are
never pooled, averaged, substituted, or used to rescue one another.

### 19.3 AUTHOR_WAVE and final closes have exact disjoint evidence cuts

The §18 version-2 stage close is retracted. The current operation row adds the
operation transition chain and report-only history:

```text
benchmarkStageOperationCloseRow = [
  publicOperationId,benchmarkQualificationContextDigest,
  qualificationAuthorityDigest,
  terminalEvidenceSupersessionTransitionChainDigest,
  benchmarkStageObligationCensusDigest,benchmarkStageResultMatrixDigest,
  benchmarkReportOnlyResultMatrixDigest,
  benchmarkReportOnlyEvidenceHistoryDigest,
  benchmarkFinalCompletionMatrixDigestOrEmptyString,
  benchmarkWaveVerdictDigestOrEmptyString,
  benchmarkFinalVerdictDigestOrEmptyString,
  benchmarkOperationAggregateDigest,operationPerformanceVerdict
]
```

Its digest tag advances to
`v-pi-benchmark-stage-operation-close-row/2`. Define the stage close exactly:

```text
benchmarkStageCloseDigest = hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
  "v-pi-benchmark-stage-close/3",
  benchmarkHistoryStreamIdentityDigest,benchmarkStage,
  candidateSubjectId,candidateBundleDigest,ownerWaveId,
  priorBenchmarkHistoryPrefixDigest,closeEnvironmentVectorDigest,
  reportOnlyEnvironmentVectorDigest,orderedPublicOperationIds,
  orderedOperationQualificationAuthorityDigests,
  orderedOperationTerminalEvidenceTransitionChainDigests,
  orderedBenchmarkStageOperationCloseRows,
  orderedBenchmarkStageOperationCloseRowDigests,
  semanticGateReceiptDigest,
  compatibilityLaneReceiptDigestOrEmptyString,
  compatibilityLaneStageState,
  ownerOc1ChoiceAuthorityReceiptDigest,
  ownerOc1AdjudicationReceiptDigestOrEmptyString,
  ownerOc1AdjudicationStageState,
  eligibleFirstAnchorCommitOperationVectorDigestOrEmptyString,
  firstAnchorRegistrySerialCommitPlanDigestOrEmptyString,
  durableArtifactStoreDigest,stageAcceptanceState
]))))
```

The operation IDs, rows, qualification authorities, and transition-chain
digests have identical cardinality/order and project byte-for-byte.
`ownerOc1ChoiceAuthorityReceipt` is the already-ratified choice of OC-1A or
OC-1B and is non-empty at every stage; it is not a measurement or
adjudication. The eligibility vector is the exact §19.4 pre-close vector for
AUTHOR_WAVE and the literal empty string for either final stage. The serial
commit plan is the exact §19.4 non-authoritative plan for AUTHOR_WAVE and the
literal empty string for either final stage. An AUTHOR_WAVE close always binds
both non-empty digests even when eligible count is zero.

The field/predicate union is exact:

| stage | compatibility digest/state | OC-1 adjudication digest/state | acceptance predicates |
|---|---|---|---|
| `AUTHOR_WAVE` | `""` / `NOT_DUE` | `""` / `NOT_DUE` | semantic, current wave/proposal, required environments, absolute, deterministic work/resource, allocation/retention/scaling, durable retention |
| `GRADUATION_FINAL` | non-empty / `SATISFIED` or `FAILED` | non-empty / `SATISFIED` or `FAILED` | every author predicate plus compatibility and OC-1 adjudication |
| `PERFECTION_FINAL` | non-empty / `SATISFIED` or `FAILED` | non-empty / `SATISFIED` or `FAILED` | every author predicate plus compatibility and OC-1 adjudication |

At AUTHOR_WAVE any non-empty final-only digest or non-`NOT_DUE` state is
structural RED. At either final, an empty digest or `NOT_DUE` is structural
RED; `ACCEPTED` requires both states `SATISFIED`. The report-only history is
mandatory retained evidence but never an acceptance predicate. The version-2
perfection close remains forward-only but advances to
`v-pi-benchmark-perfection-close/3`; it replaces the old single OC-1 field by
the non-empty choice-authority plus adjudication digests, binds the accepted
perfection-final compatibility digest, inserts
`orderedOperationQualificationAuthorityDigests,
orderedOperationTerminalEvidenceTransitionChainDigests` immediately after
`orderedPublicOperationIds` as exact projections of the accepted perfection-
final stage close, and requires both final stage states `SATISFIED`.

### 19.4 First-anchor proposal has one accepted non-speed lifecycle

Before an accepted author-wave close, the two synthetic result rows and every
required-environment verdict use literal state `FIRST_ANCHOR_PROPOSAL`, not
`ANCHOR_ESTABLISHED`, `WAVE_GREEN`, or another speed state. For an operation
whose every required environment contains the exact fixed+immediate proposal
pair from one proposal receipt, define:

```text
waveAnchorDebtState = "FIRST_ANCHOR_PROPOSAL"
waveEnvironmentPerformanceState = "FIRST_ANCHOR_PROPOSAL"
wavePerformanceState = "FIRST_ANCHOR_PROPOSAL"
operationPerformanceVerdict = "FIRST_ANCHOR_PROPOSAL"
```

This is the sole first-anchor author-wave branch. It can produce
`stageAcceptanceState="ACCEPTED"` only when every non-speed author-wave
predicate in §19.3 passes in every required environment. It asserts no ratio,
interval, winning block, peer win, `WAVE_GREEN`, FASTER claim, compatibility
claim, or debt clearance. A measured operation cannot use this token, and a
proposal operation cannot mix it with measured wave rows.

The §18 proposal version 1 is retracted because several operation proposals
must not each publish a competing next digest from the same registry base.
The current proposal is only a prospective pair:

```text
firstAnchorRegistryProposalReceiptDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-first-anchor-registry-proposal/2",
    firstAnchorEstablishmentReceiptDigest,
    fixedAnchorRegistryDigest,immediateAnchorRegistryDigest,
    proposedFixedAnchorRegistryRow,proposedImmediateAnchorRegistryRow,
    "PROSPECTIVE_PAIR_NOT_COMMITTED"
  ]))))
```

Before the stage close is hashed, filter its already frozen operation-close
rows in operation order and define:

```text
eligibleFirstAnchorCommitOperationRow = [
  eligibleIndex,publicOperationId,benchmarkStageOperationCloseRowDigest,
  firstAnchorRegistryProposalReceiptDigest,
  orderedFixedProposalResultRowDigestsByRequiredEnvironment,
  orderedImmediateProposalResultRowDigestsByRequiredEnvironment
]

eligibleFirstAnchorCommitOperationVectorDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-eligible-first-anchor-commit-operation-vector/1",
    benchmarkHistoryStreamIdentityDigest,"AUTHOR_WAVE",
    candidateSubjectId,candidateBundleDigest,ownerWaveId,
    orderedPublicOperationIds,orderedBenchmarkStageOperationCloseRowDigests,
    eligibleOperationCount,orderedEligibleFirstAnchorCommitOperationRows
  ]))))
```

The vector contains every and only close row whose aggregate and wave verdict
use `FIRST_ANCHOR_PROPOSAL`; indices are `0..count-1`; operation IDs are unique;
and both result arrays are exact required-environment projections of that
operation's matrix. A measured, rejected, final, partial-pair, foreign, or
duplicate operation is ineligible. The AUTHOR_WAVE close binds this already-
formed vector; the vector cannot name that descendant close.

Still before the close, mechanically serialize the eligible proposals into a
non-authoritative plan. For each index define:

```text
firstAnchorRegistryPlannedSerialCommitRow = [
  eligibleIndex,publicOperationId,benchmarkStageOperationCloseRowDigest,
  firstAnchorRegistryProposalReceiptDigest,
  plannedPriorFixedAnchorRegistryDigest,
  plannedPriorImmediateAnchorRegistryDigest,
  proposedFixedAnchorRegistryRow,proposedImmediateAnchorRegistryRow,
  plannedNextFixedAnchorRegistryDigest,
  plannedNextImmediateAnchorRegistryDigest
]

firstAnchorRegistryPlannedSerialCommitRowDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-first-anchor-registry-planned-serial-commit-row/1",
    eligibleFirstAnchorCommitOperationVectorDigest,
    firstAnchorRegistryPlannedSerialCommitRow
  ]))))
```

At index zero the planned-prior digests equal the two registries frozen by
every proposal. At index `i>0` they equal the immediately prior planned row's
planned-next digests. Each planned-next digest is the exact canonical registry
obtained by appending that row's one proposed record to the applicable prior
registry; no sort, overwrite, parallel same-base append, or optional omission
exists. Freeze:

```text
firstAnchorRegistrySerialCommitPlanDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-first-anchor-registry-serial-commit-plan/1",
    benchmarkHistoryStreamIdentityDigest,"AUTHOR_WAVE",
    eligibleFirstAnchorCommitOperationVectorDigest,
    fixedAnchorRegistryDigest,immediateAnchorRegistryDigest,
    eligibleOperationCount,
    orderedFirstAnchorRegistryPlannedSerialCommitRows,
    orderedFirstAnchorRegistryPlannedSerialCommitRowDigests,
    plannedFinalFixedAnchorRegistryDigest,
    plannedFinalImmediateAnchorRegistryDigest,
    "NON_AUTHORITATIVE_PRE_CLOSE_PLAN"
  ]))))
```

The final pair equals the last planned row's next pair, or the original pair
when count is zero. Operation keys are unique and the planned-row count equals
the eligibility count. The AUTHOR_WAVE stage close binds this plan. It grants
no registry authority whether the later close is accepted or rejected.

After and only after that close is durably `ACCEPTED`, define the atomic set:

```text
firstAnchorRegistryAtomicCommitSetDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-first-anchor-registry-atomic-commit-set/1",
    acceptedAuthorWaveStageCloseDigest,
    eligibleFirstAnchorCommitOperationVectorDigest,
    firstAnchorRegistrySerialCommitPlanDigest,
    fixedAnchorRegistryDigest,immediateAnchorRegistryDigest,
    eligibleOperationCount,
    orderedFirstAnchorRegistryPlannedSerialCommitRows,
    orderedFirstAnchorRegistryPlannedSerialCommitRowDigests,
    plannedFinalFixedAnchorRegistryDigest,
    plannedFinalImmediateAnchorRegistryDigest,
    "ATOMIC_COMPLETE_SET_ANCHOR_ESTABLISHED"
  ]))))
```

The atomic set must byte-match the pre-close plan in cardinality, order,
operation keys, proposal bytes, every chained prior/next pair, and final pair.
For every planned row define one operation receipt:

```text
firstAnchorRegistryCommitReceiptDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-first-anchor-registry-commit/2",
    acceptedAuthorWaveStageCloseDigest,
    eligibleFirstAnchorCommitOperationVectorDigest,
    firstAnchorRegistrySerialCommitPlanDigest,
    firstAnchorRegistryAtomicCommitSetDigest,eligibleIndex,
    publicOperationId,benchmarkStageOperationCloseRowDigest,
    firstAnchorRegistryProposalReceiptDigest,
    firstAnchorRegistryPlannedSerialCommitRowDigest,"ANCHOR_ESTABLISHED"
  ]))))
```

The accepted AUTHOR_WAVE history event names the non-empty atomic-set digest
when eligibility count is nonzero and contains a commit-receipt array with
length exactly equal to that count, where index `i` is the one receipt for
planned row `i`. When eligibility count is zero, the atomic-set field is the
exact empty string and the receipt array literal `[]`. Every REJECTED stage,
either final stage, and a perfection event also uses empty string plus `[]`.
Omission, duplication, foreign operation, wrong order, partial set, mixed
branch, zero-or-more optionality, or a commit after a rejected/non-wave close
is structural RED.

Neither the plan, accepted close, atomic set, nor individual receipt mutates a
registry before the event's successor prefix is durable. After that successor
prefix is durable, form exactly one accepted
establishment receipt per eligible operation:

```text
acceptedFirstAnchorEstablishmentReceiptDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-accepted-first-anchor-establishment/1",
    publicOperationId,candidateSubjectId,candidateBundleDigest,
    acceptedAuthorWaveStageCloseDigest,
    eligibleFirstAnchorCommitOperationVectorDigest,
    firstAnchorRegistrySerialCommitPlanDigest,
    firstAnchorRegistryAtomicCommitSetDigest,
    firstAnchorRegistryCommitReceiptDigest,
    acceptedAuthorWaveHistoryEventDigest,
    authoritySuccessorBenchmarkHistoryPrefixDigest,
    plannedFinalFixedAnchorRegistryDigest,
    plannedFinalImmediateAnchorRegistryDigest,
    "WAVE_ANCHOR_ESTABLISHED"
  ]))))
```

The receipt's outer state is exactly `WAVE_ANCHOR_ESTABLISHED`; its registry
state is exactly `ANCHOR_ESTABLISHED`. Both are authority/existence states,
not delayed speed claims. It is usable only by a later final or candidate
context whose prior prefix contains that event.

Every final environment chooses exactly one closed anchor mode:

```text
ESTABLISHED_PAIR = [
  acceptedFirstAnchorEstablishmentReceiptDigest,
  fixedEstablishedRegistryRowDigest,immediateEstablishedRegistryRowDigest,
  "WAVE_ANCHOR_ESTABLISHED","ANCHOR_ESTABLISHED_NO_SPEED_CLAIM"
]

MEASURED_WAVE_GREEN_PAIR = [
  acceptedFixedWaveResultRowDigest,acceptedImmediateWaveResultRowDigest,
  acceptedAuthorWaveVerdictDigest,"WAVE_GREEN"
]
```

`ESTABLISHED_PAIR` requires both fixed and immediate registry rows from the
same accepted establishment receipt and leaves every measured-wave field the
exact empty string. `MEASURED_WAVE_GREEN_PAIR` requires both original rows
from one accepted author-wave matrix and literal `WAVE_GREEN`, and leaves
every establishment field empty. A mixed pair, two commits, one established
and one measured anchor, or treating establishment as a win is structural
RED. The final completion receipt may therefore name an ancestor accepted
author-wave close whose original close state is `FIRST_ANCHOR_PROPOSAL` and
whose exact post-close ancestry supplies `WAVE_ANCHOR_ESTABLISHED`, or an
ancestor measured `WAVE_GREEN` close; its old unconditional WAVE_GREEN-only
test is retracted.

### 19.5 Evidence-only recovery is same-scope, acyclic, and reachable without a plan

Section 18.2's version-3 authorization/transition prose and context version 3
are retracted. To avoid a context↔transition cycle, first form one context
core from the exact static bytes:

```text
benchmarkQualificationContextCoreDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-benchmark-qualification-context-core/1",
    publicOperationId,candidateSubjectId,candidateBundleDigest,
    completePhysicalPeerCensusDigest,peerCellDispositionPartitionDigest,
    orderedExecutablePeerCellsDigest,
    orderedBaseTerminalDispositionReceiptDigests,
    peerCellPartitionBindingDigest,roleQualificationObligationProjectionDigest,
    completeQualificationExecutionCellProjectionDigest,
    qualificationKindClassPartitionVectorDigest,
    executionCellClassGroupPeerMappingDigest,
    obligationResolutionMappingDigest,
    combinedQualificationEnvironmentScopeVectorDigest,
    registryAuthorityHistoryPrefixDigest,
    acceptedAnchorRegistryCommitDigestOrEmptyString
  ]))))
```

`orderedBaseTerminalDispositionReceiptDigests` are the immutable pre-chain
static receipts. The existing operation-wide no-observation proof version 2
remains mandatory and names the old core's exact partition, class/group map,
combined scope, complete stores, and common snapshot cut.

An evidence-only authorization and transition are now exactly:

```text
terminalEvidenceSupersessionAuthorizationDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-terminal-evidence-supersession-authorization/4",
    supersessionReasonCode,candidateSubjectId,candidateBundleDigest,
    publicOperationId,oldBenchmarkQualificationContextDigest,
    oldBenchmarkQualificationContextCoreDigest,
    newBenchmarkQualificationContextCoreDigest,
    oldCombinedQualificationEnvironmentScopeVectorDigest,
    newCombinedQualificationEnvironmentScopeVectorDigest,
    "BYTE_IDENTICAL_ENVIRONMENT_SCOPE",
    oldPeerCellPartitionBindingDigest,newPeerCellPartitionBindingDigest,
    operationWideNoObservationProofDigest,orderedEvidenceRepairRows,
    challengeADigest,challengeBDigest,rootDispositionDigest,
    "AUTHORIZED_OPERATION_WIDE_PRE_OBSERVATION"
  ]))))

terminalEvidenceSupersessionTransitionDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-terminal-evidence-supersession-transition/4",
    transitionOrdinal,priorTransitionChainDigest,
    terminalEvidenceSupersessionAuthorizationDigest,
    oldBenchmarkQualificationContextDigest,
    oldBenchmarkQualificationContextCoreDigest,
    newBenchmarkQualificationContextCoreDigest,
    oldCombinedQualificationEnvironmentScopeVectorDigest,
    newCombinedQualificationEnvironmentScopeVectorDigest,
    "BYTE_IDENTICAL_ENVIRONMENT_SCOPE",
    oldPeerCellPartitionBindingDigest,newPeerCellPartitionBindingDigest,
    "APPEND_ONLY_OPERATION_CONTEXT_CORE_TRANSITION"
  ]))))

terminalEvidenceSupersessionTransitionChainDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-terminal-evidence-supersession-transition-chain/2",
    candidateSubjectId,publicOperationId,
    combinedQualificationEnvironmentScopeVectorDigest,
    transitionCount,orderedTerminalEvidenceSupersessionTransitionDigests
  ]))))
```

The old and new combined-scope digests must be byte-identical; the literal is
not a substitute for the equality check. Transition count equals array
length, ordinals are exact, and every link names the immediately prior chain
and context/core. Genesis is count `0`, literal `[]`, and the frozen empty-
chain prior digest. A different required or report-only row, class, receipt,
order, or vector digest is ineligible for this path and requires a separate
ordinary causal-source/E-3 environment amendment. It cannot reuse the old
attempt, seed, observation, result, loss, or qualification identity.

After the chain exists, choose exactly one qualification-authority branch:

```text
directQualificationAuthorityDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-qualification-authority/1","DIRECT_GENESIS",
    benchmarkQualificationContextCoreDigest,
    combinedQualificationEnvironmentScopeVectorDigest,
    genesisTerminalEvidenceSupersessionTransitionChainDigest,0,[]
  ]))))

evidenceSupersessionQualificationAuthorityDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-qualification-authority/1","EVIDENCE_SUPERSESSION",
    benchmarkQualificationContextCoreDigest,
    combinedQualificationEnvironmentScopeVectorDigest,
    terminalEvidenceSupersessionTransitionChainDigest,
    transitionCount,
    orderedTerminalEvidenceSupersessionAuthorizationDigests,
    orderedTerminalEvidenceSupersessionTransitionDigests
  ]))))
```

`qualificationAuthorityDigest` is the direct digest exactly when count is
zero and no evidence repair occurred. Its chain is the canonical empty
genesis chain. It is the evidence-supersession digest exactly when count is
positive; the authorization array is the exact transition projection in
ordinal order, and the last transition's new core equals the named current
core. The branches are disjoint and exhaustive. An empty-chain
`EVIDENCE_SUPERSESSION`, a non-empty-chain `DIRECT_GENESIS`, an omitted
authorization, or a detached transition is structural RED.

With that authority, wrap every static preflight disposition:

```text
chainScopedPeerCellPreflightDispositionReceiptDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-chain-scoped-peer-cell-preflight-disposition/1",
    benchmarkQualificationContextCoreDigest,
    qualificationAuthorityDigest,
    terminalEvidenceSupersessionTransitionChainDigest,
    physicalCensusIndex,canonicalPeerCensusRow,
    basePeerCellPreflightDispositionReceiptDigest,
    peerCellPreflightDispositionState
  ]))))
```

The ordered scoped-receipt vector is a byte-for-byte census projection used by
downstream source-resolution wrappers and direct sources. It is not fed back
into the static context. The current context is:

```text
benchmarkQualificationContextDigest =
  hex(SHA256(UTF8(JSON.stringify([
    "v-pi-benchmark-qualification-context/4",
    benchmarkQualificationContextCoreDigest,
    combinedQualificationEnvironmentScopeVectorDigest,
    registryAuthorityHistoryPrefixDigest,
    acceptedAnchorRegistryCommitDigestOrEmptyString
  ]))))
```

The context binds the complete environment scope but not the transition chain
or its authority. The transition binds the new core, never this descendant
full context, so no digest cycle exists. Static core mappings continue to bind
their immutable base receipts. Every post-authority direct/no-plan preflight
wrapper, source, and result uses the chain-scoped preflight receipt instead of
a bare base receipt and separately binds the qualification authority.

The following current families bind
`qualificationAuthorityDigest` and
`terminalEvidenceSupersessionTransitionChainDigest` directly in addition to
their context ancestry: batch plan version 6; every environment scope
disposition and wrapper; every static-terminal, required-unavailable,
report-only-unavailable, resource-blocked, first-anchor-proposal, pilot-
invalid, T9-invalid, and exhaustion source; required result envelope version
4; report-only result version 1; required stage-result matrix version 3;
report-only matrix/history version 1; operation aggregate version 3; stage
operation close row version 2; stage close version 3; and history event
version 3. A direct/no-plan result must name its exact chain-scoped preflight
receipt and cannot rely on a missing plan to carry provenance.

The required result version 4 and stage-matrix version 3 retain their exact
§18 version-3/version-2 fields in order and insert
`qualificationAuthorityDigest,terminalEvidenceSupersessionTransitionChainDigest`
immediately after `benchmarkQualificationContextDigest`. The operation
aggregate version 3 makes the identical insertion in §18's version-2 array.
History event version 3 retains §18's version-2 fields in order and inserts,
immediately after `priorBenchmarkHistoryPrefixDigest`, the exact
`orderedOperationQualificationAuthorityDigests,
orderedOperationTerminalEvidenceTransitionChainDigests` from the bound stage
close, followed by
`firstAnchorRegistryAtomicCommitSetDigestOrEmptyString` immediately before its
ordered commit-receipt array. A perfection event projects the accepted
perfection-stage authority/chain vectors and has an empty atomic-set field.
Thus plans, direct results, matrices, closes, and history all reach the same
retained no-observation proof, authorization, transition array, and chain even
when an operation has no executable group. Detached repair provenance is
structural RED.

### 19.6 Exact fourteenth-repair instructions to the ADDENDA-06 writer
